840	
841	- **Projects** (`vitest.config.ts`): `unit` runs in node. `integration` loads `wrangler.jsonc` with `LLM_MODE=mock` and a valid test `DEMO_BASE_URL`, and with `remoteBindings: false`, so the AI binding never goes remote: tests need no login and spend no neurons. `demo` loads `demo-service/wrangler.jsonc`.
842	- **Fake internet (WP-09):** the integration project's `outboundService` is an inline `fake-internet` Worker. It answers DoH for `*.example.com` (NXDOMAIN for `nxdomain.example.com`), returns 500 for `broken.example.com` and serves HTML for other `*.example.com` hosts, so no integration test touches the real network. `diagnosis.test.ts` runs real local Workflows through `introspectWorkflow`; `plan.test.ts` covers every limit with a fake `io`.
843	- Unit tests load the recorded fixtures as JSON imports and the `.sse` files with Vite `?raw` imports (`test/unit/raw.d.ts`), because the worker tsconfig has no Node types.
844	- **Demo stub:** the test runtime won't start while a service binding points at a Worker it doesn't know about. So `integration` adds a small inline stand-in `incident-copilot-demo` Worker behind `DEMO`. It keeps modes in memory, records every `setModes`, and exposes the record as `calls(ws)`. `demo-api.test.ts` and `approval.test.ts` assert what the main Worker asks it to do (e.g. `setModes`). The real demo service is tested only in `demo`.
845	- Tests use `env` and `exports` from `cloudflare:workers` (the plugin's `cloudflare:test` `env`/`SELF` are deprecated). Workflow tests use the plugin's Workflow introspection APIs. Retrying events with `waitForEvent` and the timeout path are verified in WP-02.
846	
847	## 15. CI/CD and IaC
848	
849	- **`ci.yml`** (on PRs into `main`, and reused by `deploy.yml`): `pnpm i --frozen-lockfile`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test`, then `terraform fmt -check` and `terraform init -backend=false && terraform validate` (D15). *As built (WP-15):* Node 22; the Terraform steps run only when `infra/` has `.tf` files (`hashFiles`), so they turn on by themselves if WP-25 is built. A newer push to a PR cancels the older check; a deploy's check is never cancelled. The tests need no `.dev.vars` (checked by running the suite without it).
850	- **`deploy.yml`** (on push to `main`): runs the tests, `wrangler d1 migrations apply DB --remote` and `vite build`, deploys `demo-service`, then deploys main, then runs a smoke test.
851	  - Deploys use `cloudflare/wrangler-action@v3` with `packageManager: pnpm`, so the repo's pinned wrangler runs. Concurrency group `deploy-production`, never cancelled. The job runs in the `production` environment, whose URL is the main Worker's `deployment-url`.
852	  - The smoke test calls `GET /api/health`, then `GET /api/session`, then `POST /api/demo/mode {modes:["healthy"]}`, then `POST /api/incidents "diagnose demo"`. It polls the snapshot until `activeRunId` is null and expects `category = healthy`. Run it with `LLM_MODE` live but only once per deploy, or skip the LLM with a `?smoke=rule` flag that forces `rule_based`.
853	  - *As built (WP-15):* `scripts/smoke.ts` (`pnpm smoke <url>`; Node 22 runs the `.ts` file directly, so it has no imports) always uses `?smoke=rule`, so a deploy costs no neurons. Health retries 5 times, 3 s apart (WP-13 saw one transient `1042`), and the run must finish within 90 s. The URL comes from wrangler's `deployment-url`, with an `APP_URL` repository variable as the fallback, so no URL is committed (D2). `POST /api/incidents?smoke=rule` passes `mode: "rule_based"` into `init`, `startRun` stores it in `runs.mode`, and `getRunContext` returns it (WP-17's budget guard will set the same field). The flag is public, but it can only make a run cheaper. Locally, `pnpm smoke http://localhost:5173` passes against `pnpm dev`, with no LLM call.
854	- **Secrets:** `CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN` and `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID` live in GitHub. `WEBHOOK_KEY` is set once with `wrangler secret put`. The token uses the "Edit Cloudflare Workers" template plus **Account › D1 › Edit** (for the migrations).
855	- **First deploy (WP-13, by hand from `dev`):**
856	  1. `wrangler d1 migrations apply DB --remote`.
857	  2. `pnpm build`, which writes both Workers to `dist/`.
858	  3. **Demo first:** `wrangler deploy -c dist/incident_copilot_demo/wrangler.json`. The main Worker's `DEMO` binding needs the demo to exist.
859	  4. Set `DEMO_BASE_URL` in `wrangler.jsonc` to the deployed demo.
860	  5. `pnpm build`, then `wrangler deploy`. `.wrangler/deploy/config.json` points it at `dist/incident_copilot/wrangler.json`.
861	
862	  Results: the main Worker is about 485 KiB gzipped with a 53 ms start-up; the demo is 26 KiB. `.dev.vars` (and with it `SSRF_DEV_ALLOW_ORIGIN`) is never uploaded. The first request after the deploy got one transient `1042`; it didn't happen again.
863	- **CPU (R9), from `wrangler tail --format json` during the live walkthrough:** 310 events, none cut off (all `ok`, plus `canceled` for closed sockets and the paused approval wait).
864	  - The demo Worker peaked at 2 ms.
865	  - The main Worker's HTTP routes stayed ≤ 8 ms.
866	  - Inside the Durable Object and the Workflow, CPU went above 10 ms: `narrate` 182 ms (streaming), the Workflow `run` 49 ms, agent init and snapshot calls up to 41 ms, `afterAction` 21 ms, a monitor alarm 13 ms. Nothing failed, but `narrate` is the hottest path; watch it if Error 1102 ever shows up.
867	- **Terraform (`infra/`, local state, gitignored):** Cloudflare provider v5 with `cloudflare_d1_database`, plus the AI Gateway resource if the provider supports it. Otherwise the gateway is created in the dashboard and the README says so (R1). The `database_id` output is copied into `wrangler.jsonc` once. Wrangler owns everything else (§5.1).
868	
869	## 16. LLM eval (`eval/run.ts`)
870	
871	`pnpm eval --base <url>` works like this:
872	1. Get a session cookie.
873	2. For each case (`slow` → `high_latency`, `errors` → `origin_5xx`, `redirect_loop` → `redirect_loop`, `bad_cache` → `cache_misconfiguration`, `healthy` → `healthy`): set the mode, create an incident with "what's wrong with my demo service?", and poll the snapshot until it's done.
874	3. Score each case on three checks: ① the category is correct, ② at least one citation points to a decisive check that exists (`http-*`), ③ `restore_demo_service` is proposed (for healthy, it must **not** be proposed).
875	4. Write `eval/results.md` with a table and the model used. The target is at least 4 of 5 passing.
876	
877	A full run costs about 3k neurons, so don't run it on a review day.
878	
879	*As built (WP-20):*
880	- **Files:** `eval/cases.ts` (cases, `score`, `renderResults`; pure and import-free, unit-tested in `test/unit/eval-score.test.ts`) and `eval/run.ts` (`pnpm eval --base <url> [--note "<text>"]`, Node type stripping like `scripts/smoke.ts`). Not part of CI.
881	- **Scoring:** ② needs an `http-*` ID in `findings.evidence` whose check finished (not pending or `unavailable`). ③ needs a `restore_demo_service` approval card; for `healthy`, neither a card nor the action in the findings. A `rule_based` run fails whatever it found, since the eval measures the AI.
882	- **Who made the findings:** `run_finished` now records `source` (`model`, `model+rules` or `rules`) and `fallbackTurns` (turns glm answered), from `loop/plan.ts`. No schema change.
883	- **Run limit:** a 429 waits `retryAfter` and retries (3 tries). The demo is set back to `healthy` at the end; the pending cards wait out their timeout.
884	- **First run (2026-10-16, local `pnpm dev` with the live model):** 5/5, 642 neurons (about 130 per case, well under the 3k estimate). `redirect_loop` was `model+rules`: an invalid `submit_findings` was filled in by the rules. Check ③ is mostly guaranteed by `pickAction` (WP-12). Results in `eval/results.md`.
885	
886	## 17. Build plan (2 days)
887	
888	The estimates are focused hours with heavy AI help. **Day 1 is heavy (about 14 h).** If it runs over, WP-12 and WP-13 move to the start of Day 2 ahead of all Should items. Commit after each WP, and export the session transcript into `prompts/` when the day ends.

929	## 18. Risks (in addition to PRD §15)
930	
931	| Risk | Mitigation |
932	|---|---|
933	| The gpt-oss-120b tool-call format on Workers AI differs from what's expected | *Resolved in WP-02 (§7.4.1):* both models use chat completions. Remaining risk: gpt skips `submit_findings` unless `tool_choice:"required"` is sent, and a forced turn can take about 19 s. |
934	| `waitForApproval` timeout semantics, or whether DO RPC counts toward subrequests | Timeout checked locally in WP-02 (§8.2): it throws a catchable error. Subrequest counting is from the docs only, so the budget stays at 45 with conservative counting. |
935	| Kumo 2.x changes quickly | Pin the exact version and read `ai/USAGE.md` before building UI |
936	| Day 1 runs over | WP-12/13 move to the start of Day 2, and the cut line absorbs the difference |
937	| The URL is exposed before WP-17 (D2) | Don't share it. `LLM_MODE` can be set to `mock` on the deployed Worker if abuse shows up. |
938	| The workspace ID leaks through the demo URL (D7) | Documented as accepted. Changing to a random slug later only touches `demo.ts`, the `DemoAdmin` keying and `restore_demo_service` eligibility. |

```
import type { Fetch } from "../lib/doh";

export type ToolName = "dns_lookup" | "http_probe" | "cert_info" | "provider_status";

/** Matches `CheckView.status` once finished (TDD §6.3). */
export type CheckStatus = "pass" | "fail" | "error" | "unavailable";

/**
 * `ok` means the tool ran and produced evidence about the target (status pass or fail).
 * `error` is a refusal (SSRF, scope); `unavailable` means we couldn't check (rate limit, budget, resolver down).
 */
export type ToolResult<Raw = unknown> = {
  evidenceId: string;
  tool: ToolName;
  ok: boolean;
  status: CheckStatus;
  summary: string;
  /** Compact JSON for the LLM, at most `DIGEST_MAX` bytes. */
  digest: string;
  /** Full evidence for the UI (`getEvidence`); never sent to the LLM. */
  raw: Raw;
};

export type ToolContext = {
  fetch: Fetch;
  evidenceId: string;
  /** Dev-only SSRF exception (TDD §11). */
  allowOrigin?: string;
  /** Called before every probe sample; backed by PROBE_LIMIT (`safety/quota.ts`). Returns false when limited. */
  rateLimit?: (host: string) => Promise<boolean>;
  now?: () => number;
};
import { z } from "zod";
import { toolParameters } from "../lib/schema";
import { countingFetch, SubrequestCounter } from "../loop/budget";
import { submitFindingsTool } from "../loop/findings";
import { assertInScope, OutOfScopeError, type Scope } from "../safety/origin";
import { dnsInput, runDnsLookup } from "./dns";
import { HTTP_PROBE_CAP, httpInput, runHttpProbe } from "./http";
import type { ToolContext, ToolName, ToolResult } from "./types";

/** Evidence ID prefixes (TDD §6.4); the incident's agent allocates `n` (WP-08). */
export const EVIDENCE_PREFIX: Record<ToolName, string> = {
  dns_lookup: "dns",
  http_probe: "http",
  cert_info: "cert",
  provider_status: "status",
};

export function evidenceId(tool: ToolName, n: number): string {
  return `${EVIDENCE_PREFIX[tool]}-${n}`;
}

const LABEL_FALLBACK: Record<ToolName, string> = {
  dns_lookup: "DNS lookup",
  http_probe: "HTTP probe",
  cert_info: "Certificate",
  provider_status: "Provider status",
};

/** Display name for a check; cert/provider tools aren't registered until WP-21. */
export function toolLabel(tool: ToolName): string {
  return TOOLS[tool]?.label ?? LABEL_FALLBACK[tool];
}

type ToolDef<S extends z.ZodType> = {
  name: ToolName;
  label: string;
  description: string;
  input: S;
  maxSubrequests: (input: z.output<S>) => number;
  run: (input: z.output<S>, ctx: ToolContext) => Promise<ToolResult>;
};

type Prepared = { ok: true; args: unknown; cap: number; run: (ctx: ToolContext) => Promise<ToolResult> } | { ok: false; error: string };

export type RegisteredTool = {
  name: ToolName;
  label: string;
  description: string;
  parameters: () => Record<string, unknown>;
  prepare: (args: unknown) => Prepared;
};

function register<S extends z.ZodType>(def: ToolDef<S>): RegisteredTool {
  return {
    name: def.name,
    label: def.label,
    description: def.description,
    parameters: () => toolParameters(def.input),
    prepare: (args) => {
      const parsed = def.input.safeParse(args);
      if (!parsed.success) return { ok: false, error: z.prettifyError(parsed.error) };
      const input = parsed.data;
      return { ok: true, args: input, cap: def.maxSubrequests(input), run: (ctx) => def.run(input, ctx) };
    },
  };
}

export const TOOLS: Partial<Record<ToolName, RegisteredTool>> = {
  dns_lookup: register({
    name: "dns_lookup",
    label: "DNS lookup",
    description:
      "Resolve DNS records for the incident host (or a CNAME target from earlier DNS evidence) via Cloudflare and Google DNS-over-HTTPS. Reports status, answers, TTLs, the CNAME chain and whether the resolvers disagree.",
    input: dnsInput,
    maxSubrequests: (i) => 2 * i.types.length,
    run: runDnsLookup,
  }),
  http_probe: register({
    name: "http_probe",
    label: "HTTP probe",
    description:
      "Request a URL on the incident host 3 times. Reports status codes, time to first byte and total time, redirect hops, selected headers, an untrusted body snippet and the cache headers of one same-origin asset.",
    input: httpInput,
    maxSubrequests: () => HTTP_PROBE_CAP,
    run: runHttpProbe,
  }),
};

/**
 * OpenAI chat-completions tool definitions for planning turns (TDD §7.4.1), ending with `submit_findings` (D5).
 * `finishOnly` offers just `submit_findings` for the last turn: with `tool_choice: "required"` the model must
 * finish (live check: glm ignored "call submit_findings now" and asked for more probes).
 */
export function llmTools({ finishOnly = false } = {}) {
  if (finishOnly) return [submitFindingsTool()];
  return [
    ...Object.values(TOOLS).map((t) => ({
      type: "function" as const,
      function: { name: t.name, description: t.description, parameters: t.parameters() },
    })),
    submitFindingsTool(),
  ];
}

export type RunOutcome = { ok: true; result: ToolResult; subrequests: number; cap: number } | { ok: false; invalid: string };

/**
 * Validates args (zod), enforces the origin pin (D6) and runs the tool under its subrequest cap.
 * Invalid or out-of-scope calls come back as `invalid` so the loop can report them to the model (FR-12).
 */
export async function runTool(name: string, args: unknown, ctx: ToolContext, scope: Scope): Promise<RunOutcome> {
  const tool = TOOLS[name as ToolName];
  if (!tool) return { ok: false, invalid: `Unknown tool ${name}.` };
  const prepared = tool.prepare(args);
  if (!prepared.ok) return { ok: false, invalid: `Invalid arguments for ${name}: ${prepared.error}` };
  try {
    assertInScope(name, prepared.args, scope);
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof OutOfScopeError) return { ok: false, invalid: e.message };
    throw e;
  }
  const counter = new SubrequestCounter(prepared.cap);
  const result = await prepared.run({ ...ctx, fetch: countingFetch(ctx.fetch, counter) });
  return { ok: true, result, subrequests: counter.count, cap: prepared.cap };
}
export const DIGEST_MAX = 1536;

const encoder = new TextEncoder();
const byteLength = (s: string) => encoder.encode(s).length;

/**
 * Serialises `obj` as compact JSON, dropping keys in `dropOrder` until it fits in `max` bytes.
 * As a last resort the string is cut, so the bound always holds.
 */
export function fitDigest(obj: Record<string, unknown>, dropOrder: readonly string[], max = DIGEST_MAX): string {
  const copy: Record<string, unknown> = { ...obj };
  let out = JSON.stringify(copy);
  for (const key of dropOrder) {
    if (byteLength(out) <= max) return out;
    delete copy[key];
    out = JSON.stringify(copy);
  }
  while (byteLength(out) > max) out = `${out.slice(0, Math.max(0, out.length - 64))}…`;
  return out;
}

/**
 * Text from the internet, prepared for a prompt: tags stripped, whitespace collapsed, cut to `max`
 * characters and wrapped so the model treats it as data (TDD §9.1). Nested markers are escaped.
 */
export function untrusted(text: string, max = 500): string {
  const plain = text
    .replace(/<script[\s\S]*?<\/script>|<style[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi, " ")
    .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim()
    .slice(0, max)
    .replace(/<\/?untrusted/gi, (m) => m.replace("<", "&lt;"));
  return `<untrusted>${plain}</untrusted>`;
}
/**
 * Origin pin (D6): tools only touch the incident's own host, plus CNAME targets found in this
 * incident's DNS evidence for DNS/cert lookups. A violation counts as an invalid call (FR-12).
 */
export type Scope = { host: string; cnameTargets: readonly string[] };

export class OutOfScopeError extends Error {
  override name = "OutOfScopeError";
}

const normHost = (h: string) => h.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\.$/, "");

function hostOf(url: unknown): string {
  if (typeof url !== "string") throw new OutOfScopeError("http_probe needs a url string.");
  try {
    return normHost(new URL(url).hostname);
  } catch {
    throw new OutOfScopeError(`${url} isn't a valid URL.`);
  }
}

export function assertInScope(tool: string, args: unknown, scope: Scope): void {
  const incidentHost = normHost(scope.host);
  const a = (args ?? {}) as { url?: unknown; hostname?: unknown };

  switch (tool) {
    case "http_probe": {
      const host = hostOf(a.url);
      if (host !== incidentHost) {
        throw new OutOfScopeError(`http_probe is limited to ${incidentHost}; ${host} needs its own incident.`);
      }
      return;
    }
    case "dns_lookup":
    case "cert_info": {
      if (typeof a.hostname !== "string") throw new OutOfScopeError(`${tool} needs a hostname string.`);
      const host = normHost(a.hostname);
      const allowed = new Set([incidentHost, ...scope.cnameTargets.map(normHost)]);
      if (!allowed.has(host)) {
        throw new OutOfScopeError(`${tool} is limited to ${incidentHost} and its CNAME targets; ${host} isn't one.`);
      }
      return;
    }
    case "provider_status":
    case "submit_findings":
      return;
    default:
      throw new OutOfScopeError(`Unknown tool ${tool}.`);
  }
}
/** Minimal fetch signature so callers can pass a counting or fake fetch. */
export type Fetch = (input: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>;

export const RR_TYPES = { A: 1, NS: 2, CNAME: 5, MX: 15, TXT: 16, AAAA: 28 } as const;
export type RrType = keyof typeof RR_TYPES;

export const RESOLVERS = {
  cloudflare: "https://cloudflare-dns.com/dns-query",
  google: "https://dns.google/resolve",
} as const;

export type DohAnswer = { name: string; type: number; ttl: number; data: string };
/** `status` is the DNS RCODE: 0 NOERROR, 2 SERVFAIL, 3 NXDOMAIN. */
export type DohResult = { status: number; answers: DohAnswer[] };

const DOH_TIMEOUT_MS = 5_000;

type DohJson = { Status?: number; Answer?: { name: string; type: number; TTL: number; data: string }[] };

/** One DNS-over-HTTPS JSON query. Throws on HTTP or network errors; callers decide how to fail. */
export async function dohQuery(fetchFn: Fetch, resolver: string, name: string, type: RrType): Promise<DohResult> {
  const url = new URL(resolver);
  url.searchParams.set("name", name);
  url.searchParams.set("type", type);
  const res = await fetchFn(url.toString(), {
    headers: { accept: "application/dns-json" },
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(DOH_TIMEOUT_MS),
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`DoH ${resolver} returned HTTP ${res.status}`);
  const json: DohJson = await res.json();
  return {
    status: json.Status ?? -1,
    answers: (json.Answer ?? []).map((a) => ({ name: a.name, type: a.type, ttl: a.TTL, data: a.data })),
  };
}
```

```
import { HIGH_LATENCY_TOTAL_MS, HIGH_LATENCY_TTFB_MS } from "../loop/budget";
import type { DnsRaw } from "../tools/dns";
import { isTlsError, type HttpRaw, type Sample } from "../tools/http";
import type { ToolName, ToolResult } from "../tools/types";
import type { Category, CertRaw, Confidence, Findings, Provider, ProviderRaw } from "./categories";

type Ev<Raw> = { id: string; raw: Raw; summary: string };
type Evidence = {
  dns: Ev<DnsRaw> | null;
  http: Ev<HttpRaw> | null;
  cert: Ev<CertRaw> | null;
  status: Ev<ProviderRaw> | null;
};
type Match = { category: Category; confidence: Confidence; ids: string[]; cause: string };

const EDGE_STATUSES = new Set([520, 521, 522, 523, 524, 525, 526, 527, 530]);
const CERT_WARN_DAYS = 7;
const PROVIDER_NAMES: Record<Provider, string> = { cloudflare: "Cloudflare", github: "GitHub", aws: "AWS" };

const NEXT_STEPS: Record<Category, string[]> = {
  dns_failure: [
    "Check that the domain is registered and its nameservers are correct at the registrar.",
    "Check that the zone has A or AAAA records for this hostname.",
  ],
  dns_mismatch: [
    "Compare the records at each authoritative nameserver; a recent change may still be propagating.",
    "Check for a DNSSEC or delegation problem that one resolver rejects.",
  ],
  tls_expiry: [
    "Renew the certificate or check that automatic renewal is working.",
    "Make sure the origin serves the full certificate chain for this hostname.",
  ],
  origin_5xx: [
    "Check the origin's application logs and recent deploys around the time errors started.",
    "Roll back the last change if the errors began after a deploy.",
  ],
  edge_error: [
    "Check that the origin server is up and reachable from Cloudflare.",
    "Check the origin's firewall, TLS mode and timeouts for the edge error code shown.",
  ],
  high_latency: [
    "Look for slow database queries or upstream calls in the origin.",
    "Check whether responses are cached at the edge and whether load has spiked.",
  ],
  redirect_loop: [
    "Check redirect rules on the origin and at the edge for rules that undo each other.",
    "Check the SSL mode: an HTTPS redirect at the origin behind a flexible edge mode loops.",
  ],
  cache_misconfiguration: [
    "Serve static assets with a public, long-lived cache-control header.",
    "Stop setting cookies on static asset responses.",
  ],
  provider_outage: [
    "Follow the provider's status page and wait for their fix.",
    "Fail over to another region or provider if you have one.",
  ],
  healthy: [
    "If users still report problems, ask for the exact URL, region and time.",
    "Add the endpoint to the watchlist to catch intermittent failures.",
  ],
  inconclusive: [
    "Run the checks again, or probe the specific path that users report as broken.",
    "Check the origin's logs for the requests made by these probes.",
  ],
};

/** Rule-based classification (TDD §9.3): first match wins. Pure, so the Workflow can call it inside a step. */
export function classify(results: ToolResult[]): Findings {
  const e = pick(results);
  const m = RULES.reduce<Match | null>((found, rule) => found ?? rule(e), null) ?? inconclusive(e);
  return {
    category: m.category,
    confidence: m.confidence,
    evidence: m.ids,
    cause: m.cause,
    next_steps: NEXT_STEPS[m.category],
    actions: m.category === "healthy" ? [] : ["restore_demo_service"],
  };
}

/** The latest result per tool that holds evidence about the target; refusals and outages are skipped. */
function pick(results: ToolResult[]): Evidence {
  const latest = <Raw>(tool: ToolName, usable: (r: ToolResult) => boolean): Ev<Raw> | null => {
    const r = results.filter((x) => x.tool === tool && usable(x)).at(-1);
    return r ? { id: r.evidenceId, raw: r.raw as Raw, summary: r.summary } : null;
  };
  return {
    dns: latest<DnsRaw>("dns_lookup", (r) => r.ok),
    http: latest<HttpRaw>("http_probe", (r) => (r.raw as HttpRaw).samples.length > 0),
    cert: latest<CertRaw>("cert_info", (r) => r.ok),
    status: latest<ProviderRaw>("provider_status", (r) => r.ok),
  };
}

const RULES: ((e: Evidence) => Match | null)[] = [
  dnsFailure,
  tlsExpiry,
  redirectLoop,
  edgeError,
  providerOutage,
  origin5xx,
  highLatency,
  cacheMisconfiguration,
  dnsMismatch,
  healthy,
];

function dnsFailure({ dns, http }: Evidence): Match | null {
  if (dns) {
    const per = Object.values(dns.raw.resolvers).map((types) => Object.values(types).filter((t) => t.status !== "ERROR"));
    if (per.some((answered) => answered.length === 0)) return null;
    const failed = per.map((answered) => answered.find((t) => t.status === "NXDOMAIN" || t.status === "SERVFAIL")?.status);
    if (failed.every(Boolean)) {
      return match("dns_failure", "high", [dns.id], `Both DNS resolvers return ${[...new Set(failed)].join("/")} for ${dns.raw.hostname}`);
    }
    const wantsAddresses = dns.raw.types.some((t) => t === "A" || t === "AAAA");
    if (wantsAddresses && Object.values(dns.raw.addresses).every((a) => a.length === 0)) {
      return match("dns_failure", "high", [dns.id], `Neither DNS resolver returns an A or AAAA record for ${dns.raw.hostname}`);
    }
    return null;
  }
  // No DNS check, but the probe's own SSRF lookup found no address.
  if (http?.raw.refused?.code === "no_addresses") {
    return match("dns_failure", "high", [http.id], `${host(http.raw)} has no A or AAAA records, so it couldn't be probed`);
  }
  return null;
}

function tlsExpiry({ http, cert }: Evidence): Match | null {
  const tls = http?.raw.samples.filter(isTlsError) ?? [];
  if (http && tls.length) {
    return match("tls_expiry", "high", [http.id], `The TLS handshake with ${host(http.raw)} fails on ${tls.length}/${http.raw.samples.length} samples`);
  }
  const certs = cert?.raw.certs.filter((c) => c.sanMatch) ?? [];
  if (!cert || certs.length === 0) return null;
  const best = certs.reduce((a, b) => (b.daysToExpiry > a.daysToExpiry ? b : a));
  if (best.daysToExpiry < 0) {
    return match("tls_expiry", "high", [cert.id], `The newest certificate for ${cert.raw.hostname} expired ${-best.daysToExpiry} day(s) ago`);
  }
  if (best.daysToExpiry <= CERT_WARN_DAYS && http && allOk(http.raw.samples)) {
    return match(
      "tls_expiry",
      "medium",
      [cert.id, http.id],
      `The site responds normally, but its newest certificate expires in ${best.daysToExpiry} day(s)`,
    );
  }
  return null;
}

function redirectLoop({ http }: Evidence): Match | null {
  const s0 = http?.raw.samples[0];
  if (!http || !s0 || (s0.stop !== "loop" && s0.stop !== "max_hops")) return null;
  const what = s0.stop === "loop" ? `loop back to a visited URL after ${s0.hops.length} hops` : `exceed the hop cap (${s0.hops.length} hops)`;
  return match("redirect_loop", "high", [http.id], `Redirects from ${http.raw.url} ${what}`);
}

function edgeError({ http }: Evidence): Match | null {
  if (!http || !/cloudflare/i.test(http.raw.headers.server ?? "")) return null;
  const edge = statuses(http.raw.samples).filter((s) => EDGE_STATUSES.has(s));
  if (edge.length < 2) return null;
  return match(
    "edge_error",
    "high",
    [http.id],
    `Cloudflare's edge returns HTTP ${[...new Set(edge)].join("/")} on ${edge.length}/${http.raw.samples.length} samples, so it can't get a good response from the origin`,
  );
}

function providerOutage({ http, dns, status }: Evidence): Match | null {
  if (!http || !status) return null;
  const failing = http.raw.samples.some((s) => s.stop === "error" || (s.status ?? 0) >= 500);
  if (!failing) return null;
  for (const { provider, via } of fingerprint(http.raw, dns?.raw)) {
    const active = status.raw.providers[provider]?.active ?? [];
    if (active.length === 0) continue;
    const ids = [http.id, status.id, ...(via === "dns" && dns ? [dns.id] : [])];
    const name = active[0]!.name.replace(/\s+/g, " ").slice(0, 80);
    return match("provider_outage", "medium", ids, `Requests are failing and ${PROVIDER_NAMES[provider]} reports an active incident ("${name}")`);
  }
  return null;
}

function origin5xx({ http }: Evidence): Match | null {
  if (!http) return null;
  const n = http.raw.samples.length;
  const bad = statuses(http.raw.samples).filter((s) => s >= 500);
  if (bad.length < 2) return null;
  const confidence = bad.length === n && n >= 3 ? "high" : "medium";
  return match("origin_5xx", confidence, [http.id], `The origin returns HTTP ${[...new Set(bad)].join("/")} on ${bad.length}/${n} samples`);
}

function highLatency({ http }: Evidence): Match | null {
  if (!http) return null;
  const { medianTotalMs: total, medianTtfbMs: ttfb } = http.raw;
  if ((total ?? 0) < HIGH_LATENCY_TOTAL_MS && (ttfb ?? 0) < HIGH_LATENCY_TTFB_MS) return null;
  return match("high_latency", "high", [http.id], `Responses are slow: median ${total} ms in total, ${ttfb} ms to first byte`);
}

function cacheMisconfiguration({ http }: Evidence): Match | null {
  const asset = http?.raw.asset;
  if (!http || !asset || !("status" in asset)) return null;
  const header = /\b(no-store|private)\b/i.exec(asset.cacheControl ?? "")?.[1]?.toLowerCase();
  if (!header && !asset.setCookie) return null;
  const problems = [header && `cache-control ${header}`, asset.setCookie && "a set-cookie header"].filter(Boolean).join(" and ");
  return match("cache_misconfiguration", "high", [http.id], `The static asset ${new URL(asset.url).pathname} is served with ${problems}, so it can't be cached`);
}

function dnsMismatch({ dns }: Evidence): Match | null {
  if (!dns?.raw.mismatch) return null;
  return match("dns_mismatch", "medium", [dns.id], `Cloudflare and Google DNS give different answers for ${dns.raw.hostname}`);
}

function healthy({ http, dns }: Evidence): Match | null {
  if (!http || !allOk(http.raw.samples)) return null;
  const ids = [http.id, ...(dns ? [dns.id] : [])];
  const s = statuses(http.raw.samples);
  return match("healthy", "high", ids, `${host(http.raw)} answers HTTP ${[...new Set(s)].join("/")} on ${s.length}/${s.length} samples in ${http.raw.medianTotalMs} ms (median)`);
}

function inconclusive({ http, dns, cert, status }: Evidence): Match {
  const ids = [http, dns, cert, status].flatMap((x) => (x ? [x.id] : []));
  if (ids.length === 0) return { category: "inconclusive", confidence: "low", ids, cause: "No check returned usable evidence, so there's nothing to diagnose yet." };
  const lead = http ?? dns!;
  return match("inconclusive", "low", ids, `The checks don't match a known failure pattern; the main signal is: ${trimDot(lead.summary)}`);
}

function match(category: Category, confidence: Confidence, ids: string[], text: string): Match {
  return { category, confidence, ids, cause: `${text} ${ids.map((id) => `[${id}]`).join(" ")}.` };
}

function fingerprint(http: HttpRaw, dns: DnsRaw | undefined): { provider: Provider; via: "http" | "dns" }[] {
  const out: { provider: Provider; via: "http" | "dns" }[] = [];
  if (/cloudflare/i.test(http.headers.server ?? "") || http.headers["cf-ray"]) out.push({ provider: "cloudflare", via: "http" });
  const cnames = dns?.cnameChain ?? [];
  if (cnames.some((c) => c.endsWith(".github.io"))) out.push({ provider: "github", via: "dns" });
  if (cnames.some((c) => c.includes("amazonaws"))) out.push({ provider: "aws", via: "dns" });
  return out;
}

const statuses = (samples: Sample[]) => samples.flatMap((s) => (s.status === null ? [] : [s.status]));
const allOk = (samples: Sample[]) => samples.length > 0 && samples.every((s) => s.status !== null && s.status >= 200 && s.status < 400);
const host = (http: HttpRaw) => new URL(http.url).hostname;
export const trimDot = (s: string) => s.trim().replace(/\.$/, "");
/** Failure categories (PRD §6.4, TDD §6.3). */
export const CATEGORIES = [
  "dns_failure",
  "dns_mismatch",
  "tls_expiry",
  "origin_5xx",
  "edge_error",
  "high_latency",
  "redirect_loop",
  "cache_misconfiguration",
  "provider_outage",
  "healthy",
  "inconclusive",
] as const;
export type Category = (typeof CATEGORIES)[number];

export const CONFIDENCES = ["high", "medium", "low"] as const;
export type Confidence = (typeof CONFIDENCES)[number];

/** One-line definitions; the planning prompt lists these (TDD §9.1). */
export const CATEGORY_INFO: Record<Category, string> = {
  dns_failure: "The name doesn't resolve: NXDOMAIN/SERVFAIL from both resolvers, or no A/AAAA records.",
  dns_mismatch: "The resolvers disagree on the response code or on whether the name has any address.",
  tls_expiry: "The TLS handshake fails or the certificate has expired (or expires within 7 days).",
  origin_5xx: "The origin returns 5xx on most samples.",
  edge_error: "Cloudflare's edge returns 520–527 or 530: it can't reach or talk to the origin.",
  high_latency: "Responses succeed but are slow (median total ≥ 2000 ms or TTFB ≥ 1500 ms).",
  redirect_loop: "Redirects loop back to a visited URL or exceed the hop cap.",
  cache_misconfiguration: "Static assets are sent with no-store/private or set cookies, so they can't be cached.",
  provider_outage: "Requests fail and the hosting provider has an active incident.",
  healthy: "Every sample succeeds (2xx/3xx) and nothing above applies.",
  inconclusive: "The evidence doesn't fit any category (timeouts with good DNS, 4xx, missing checks).",
};

/** Action catalog IDs (TDD §7.6); WP-12's `actions/catalog.ts` decides which are proposable. */
export const ACTION_IDS = ["restore_demo_service", "add_to_watchlist", "recheck_burst", "generate_postmortem"] as const;
export type ActionId = (typeof ACTION_IDS)[number];

/** What each action does and when it can be proposed (TDD §7.6); listed in the planning prompt. */
export const ACTION_INFO: Record<ActionId, string> = {
  restore_demo_service: "Switch the demo service back to healthy mode. Only for the demo target, and only when it isn't healthy.",
  add_to_watchlist: "Re-check the endpoint every 5 minutes for 24 hours. Only if it isn't already watched.",
  recheck_burst: "Re-check every minute for 15 minutes. Only when no monitor is running.",
  generate_postmortem: "Write a postmortem. Only once the incident is resolved.",
};

/** The structured diagnosis: the `submit_findings` tool's shape (D5, TDD §7.3), also produced by `classify`. */
export type Findings = {
  category: Category;
  confidence: Confidence;
  evidence: string[];
  cause: string;
  next_steps: string[];
  actions: ActionId[];
};

/** `cert_info` raw evidence (built in WP-21). */
export type CertRaw = {
  hostname: string;
  certs: { issuer: string; notBefore: string; notAfter: string; daysToExpiry: number; sanMatch: boolean }[];
};

export const PROVIDERS = ["cloudflare", "github", "aws"] as const;
export type Provider = (typeof PROVIDERS)[number];

/** `provider_status` raw evidence (built in WP-21). Providers that weren't checked are absent. */
export type ProviderRaw = {
  providers: Partial<Record<Provider, { active: { name: string; status?: string }[] }>>;
};
```

```
import type { Decision, Proposal, RunContext, VerifyResult } from "../agent/IncidentAgent";
import type { ActionOutcome } from "../actions/catalog";
import type { RunMode, RunStatus } from "../agent/state";
import type { Findings } from "../diagnosis/categories";
import { LlmUnavailable, type LlmRequest, type LlmResult, type ToolCall } from "../llm/models";
import { planningSystem, planningUser, type EvidenceLine } from "../llm/prompts";
import { assertInScope, OutOfScopeError, type Scope } from "../safety/origin";
import type { DnsRaw } from "../tools/dns";
import { llmTools, TOOLS } from "../tools/registry";
import type { ToolName, ToolResult } from "../tools/types";
import { MAX_PLAN_TURNS, MAX_TOOL_CALLS, RUN_DEADLINE_MS, SUBREQUEST_BUDGET, SubrequestBudget } from "./budget";
import { parseFindings, SUBMIT_FINDINGS } from "./findings";

/**
 * Bounds for one run (TDD §5.2). `tailReserve` keeps room for rules, findings, narrate, finish and the approval
 * tail (propose, outcome, action, verify). Each is one agent call: verify's own fetches run inside the agent.
 */
export type DiagnoseLimits = {
  maxTurns: number;
  maxTools: number;
  deadlineMs: number;
  budget: number;
  tailReserve: number;
  /** Run the baseline checks (DNS + HTTP) before the first AI turn of a new incident (WP-13b). */
  baseline: boolean;
};
export const DEFAULT_LIMITS: DiagnoseLimits = {
  maxTurns: MAX_PLAN_TURNS,
  maxTools: MAX_TOOL_CALLS,
  deadlineMs: RUN_DEADLINE_MS,
  budget: SUBREQUEST_BUDGET,
  tailReserve: 8,
  baseline: true,
};
/** gpt-oss spends part of `max_tokens` on reasoning; a cut-off submit_findings would be wasted. */
export const PLAN_MAX_TOKENS = 1500;
/** An LLM turn costs up to 2 subrequests (primary + fallback) plus 1 for `addPendingChecks`. */
const TURN_COST = 3;
/** The cheapest check: `dns_lookup` for A + AAAA on two resolvers. Below this, another tool can't fit. */
const CHEAPEST_TOOL = 4;
/** Planning-turn indexes for checks the run makes itself, so their evidence IDs are retry-safe too. */
const BASELINE_TURN = 0;
const RULES_TURN = 99;

/** The agent methods a run calls (TDD §7.2). */
export type RunAgent = {
  getRunContext(runId: string): Promise<RunContext>;
  addPendingChecks(runId: string, turn: number, calls: { tool: ToolName; args: unknown }[]): Promise<string[]>;
  recordCheck(evidenceId: string, result: ToolResult): Promise<void>;
  ruleFindings(runId: string, modelArgs: unknown): Promise<Findings>;
  setFindings(runId: string, findings: Findings, mode: RunMode): Promise<void>;
  narrate(runId: string): Promise<unknown>;
  finishRun(runId: string, status: Exclude<RunStatus, "running">, stats: FinishStats): Promise<void>;
  proposeAction(runId: string, workflowId: string): Promise<Proposal>;
  recordApprovalOutcome(approvalId: string, decision: Decision, detail?: string): Promise<void>;
  executeAction(approvalId: string): Promise<ActionOutcome>;
  afterAction(runId: string, approvalId: string): Promise<VerifyResult>;
};

export type ApprovalDecision = "approved" | "rejected" | "expired";

/**
 * Who made the findings (WP-20, for the eval): the model's valid `submit_findings`, the rules keeping the
 * valid fields of an invalid one, or the rules alone. Recorded with `fallbackTurns` on `run_finished`.
 */
export type FindingsSource = "model" | "model+rules" | "rules";
export type FinishStats = { toolCalls: number; llmTurns: number; fallbackTurns?: number; source?: FindingsSource };

/**
 * What the Workflow provides. `step` is `step.do` (durable, retried; results replay after a restart);
 * `now` is only called inside steps, so every time-based decision replays the same way.
 */
export type RunIO = {
  step<T>(name: string, fn: () => Promise<T>, opts?: { retries?: number }): Promise<T>;
  now(): number;
  agent: RunAgent;
  llm(req: LlmRequest): Promise<LlmResult>;
  tool(name: ToolName, args: unknown, evidenceId: string, scope: Scope): Promise<{ result: ToolResult; subrequests: number }>;
  /** This Workflow instance's ID, stored on the approval so the agent can resume it. */
  workflowId: string;
  /** Waits (durably, up to 24 h) for the card's decision. A rejection or a timeout is returned, not thrown. */
  waitForApproval(): Promise<ApprovalDecision>;
};

export type ValidCall = { tool: ToolName; args: unknown; cap: number };
export type Validated = { tools: ValidCall[]; findings: Findings | null; findingsArgs: unknown; invalid: string[] };

/**
 * Validates one turn's calls (FR-12): zod via `prepare`, the origin pin (D6), JSON that didn't parse,
 * and `submit_findings` (whose evidence must cite checks that exist).
 */
export function validateCalls(calls: ToolCall[], scope: Scope, knownIds: readonly string[]): Validated {
  const out: Validated = { tools: [], findings: null, findingsArgs: null, invalid: [] };
  const known = new Set(knownIds);
  for (const call of calls) {
    if (call.argsError) {
      out.invalid.push(`${call.name}: ${call.argsError}`);
      continue;
    }
    if (call.name === SUBMIT_FINDINGS) {
      out.findingsArgs = call.args;
      const parsed = parseFindings(call.args);
      if (!parsed.ok) {
        out.invalid.push(`submit_findings: ${parsed.error}`);
        continue;
      }
      const evidence = parsed.findings.evidence.filter((id) => known.has(id));
      if (evidence.length === 0) {
        out.invalid.push(`submit_findings: evidence must cite checks from this incident (${[...known].join(", ") || "none yet"}).`);
        continue;
      }
      out.findings = { ...parsed.findings, evidence };
      continue;
    }
    const tool = TOOLS[call.name as ToolName];
    if (!tool) {
      out.invalid.push(`Unknown tool ${call.name}.`);
      continue;
    }
    const prepared = tool.prepare(call.args);
    if (!prepared.ok) {
      out.invalid.push(`Invalid arguments for ${call.name}: ${prepared.error}`);
      continue;
    }
    try {
      assertInScope(call.name, prepared.args, scope);
    } catch (e) {
      if (!(e instanceof OutOfScopeError)) throw e;
      out.invalid.push(e.message);
      continue;
    }
    out.tools.push({ tool: tool.name, args: prepared.args, cap: prepared.cap });
  }
  return out;
}

type TurnResult =
  | { kind: "unavailable"; reason: string; attempts: number; at: number }
  | ({ kind: "turn"; attempts: number; at: number } & Validated);

type CheckOut = { line: EvidenceLine; tool: ToolName; cnameTargets: string[]; subrequests: number; at: number };

export type ActionSummary = { approvalId: string; decision: ApprovalDecision | "error"; verified?: boolean };
export type RunSummary = {
  status: Exclude<RunStatus, "running">;
  mode: RunMode;
  toolCalls: number;
  llmTurns: number;
  category?: string;
  action?: ActionSummary;
};

/**
 * One bounded diagnosis run (TDD §8.2). Every limit ends in findings built from what was gathered
 * ("Limits keep progress"): the turn and tool caps end with a finish-only turn; the deadline, the LLM being
 * unavailable or a second invalid call go to the rules, which keep the model's valid fields.
 */
export async function diagnose(io: RunIO, runId: string, limits: DiagnoseLimits = DEFAULT_LIMITS): Promise<RunSummary> {
  const { summary, approvalId } = await investigate(io, runId, limits);
  if (!approvalId) return summary;
  return { ...summary, action: await approvalTail(io, runId, approvalId) };
}

/** Plan, check, find, narrate, propose, finish. The run is finished before any approval wait (D13). */
async function investigate(io: RunIO, runId: string, limits: DiagnoseLimits): Promise<{ summary: RunSummary; approvalId: string | null }> {
  const stats = { toolCalls: 0, llmTurns: 0 };
  let mode: RunMode = "ai";
  try {
    const ctx = await io.step("context", async () => ({ ...(await io.agent.getRunContext(runId)), startedAt: io.now() }));
    const budget = new SubrequestBudget(limits.budget);
    budget.settle(0, 1);
    budget.reserve(limits.tailReserve);

    const deadline = ctx.startedAt + limits.deadlineMs;
    let lastAt = ctx.startedAt;
    const evidence: EvidenceLine[] = [...ctx.evidence];
    // CNAME targets found during the run widen the origin pin for dns_lookup (D6).
    const cnameTargets = [...ctx.scope.cnameTargets];
    const scope: Scope = { host: ctx.scope.host, cnameTargets };
    const ranThisRun = new Set<ToolName>();
    mode = ctx.mode;

    let findings: Findings | null = null;
    let lastFindingsArgs: unknown = null;
    let fromModel = false;
    let fallbackTurns = 0;
    let limit: Exclude<RunStatus, "running" | "done" | "failed"> | null = null;
    let invalidTurns = 0;
    let feedback: string | undefined;

    /** Runs one check in its own step. Never throws: a broken check is stored as an `error` result. */
    const runCheck = async (id: string, call: ValidCall): Promise<void> => {
      if (!budget.reserve(call.cap)) {
        limit ??= "limit_budget";
        return;
      }
      let out: CheckOut;
      try {
        out = await io.step(`tool-${id}`, async () => {
          let result: ToolResult;
          let subrequests = 0;
          try {
            ({ result, subrequests } = await io.tool(call.tool, call.args, id, scope));
          } catch (e) {
            result = errorResult(id, call.tool, e);
          }
          await io.agent.recordCheck(id, result);
          const cnameTargets = result.tool === "dns_lookup" && result.ok ? ((result.raw as DnsRaw).cnameTargets ?? []) : [];
          return { line: toLine(result), tool: call.tool, cnameTargets, subrequests, at: io.now() };
        });
      } catch {
        // The step failed even after retries (e.g. the agent RPC): count the worst case and move on.
        out = { line: toLine(errorResult(id, call.tool, new Error("check could not be recorded"))), tool: call.tool, cnameTargets: [], subrequests: call.cap, at: lastAt };
      }
      budget.settle(call.cap, out.subrequests + 1);
      stats.toolCalls++;
      lastAt = out.at;
      evidence.push(out.line);
      ranThisRun.add(out.tool);
      for (const t of out.cnameTargets) if (!cnameTargets.includes(t)) cnameTargets.push(t);
    };

    /** The checks every diagnosis needs: DNS for the host (skipped for IP literals) and HTTP for the target. */
    const baselineCalls = (): ValidCall[] =>
      (
        [
          ["dns_lookup", { hostname: scope.host }],
          ["http_probe", { url: ctx.target }],
        ] as const
      )
        .filter(([tool]) => !ranThisRun.has(tool))
        .flatMap(([tool, args]) => {
          const prepared = TOOLS[tool]?.prepare(args);
          return prepared?.ok ? [{ tool, args: prepared.args, cap: prepared.cap }] : [];
        });
    const runBaseline = async (turn: number, stepName: string) => {
      const calls = baselineCalls();
      if (!calls.length || lastAt >= deadline || budget.remaining() < 1 + Math.min(...calls.map((c) => c.cap))) return;
      const ids = await io.step(stepName, () => io.agent.addPendingChecks(runId, turn, calls.map(({ tool, args }) => ({ tool, args }))));
      budget.settle(0, 1);
      for (const [i, call] of calls.entries()) {
        if (lastAt >= deadline) break;
        await runCheck(ids[i]!, call);
      }
    };

    // WP-13b: live models planned one check per turn, so every run spent a turn per check and hit the
    // turn cap. A new incident always needs DNS + HTTP, so they run first (the first card shows in about
    // a second), and the model usually submits findings on its first turn.
    if (limits.baseline && ctx.kind === "initial") await runBaseline(BASELINE_TURN, "pending-baseline");

    if (mode === "ai") {
      for (let turn = 1; turn <= limits.maxTurns; turn++) {
        if (lastAt >= deadline) {
          limit ??= "limit_deadline";
          break;
        }
        if (budget.remaining() < TURN_COST) {
          limit ??= "limit_budget";
          break;
        }
        const toolsLeft = limits.maxTools - stats.toolCalls;
        const finishReason =
          turn === limits.maxTurns ? "limit_turns" : toolsLeft <= 0 ? "limit_tools" : budget.remaining() < TURN_COST + CHEAPEST_TOOL ? "limit_budget" : null;
        const knownIds = evidence.map((e) => e.evidenceId);
        const r = await io.step(`llm-turn-${turn}`, async (): Promise<TurnResult> => {
          try {
            const res = await io.llm({
              system: planningSystem({ target: ctx.target, toolsLeft: finishReason ? 0 : toolsLeft, turnsLeft: limits.maxTurns - turn + 1, isDemo: ctx.isDemo }),
              messages: planningUser({ question: ctx.question, evidence, ...(feedback ? { invalid: feedback } : {}) }),
              tools: llmTools({ finishOnly: !!finishReason }),
              toolChoice: "required",
              maxTokens: PLAN_MAX_TOKENS,
            });
            const v = validateCalls(res.toolCalls, scope, knownIds);
            if (res.toolCalls.length === 0) v.invalid.push("No tool call. Always call a tool or submit_findings.");
            return { kind: "turn", ...v, attempts: res.fallback ? 2 : 1, at: io.now() };
          } catch (e) {
            if (e instanceof LlmUnavailable) return { kind: "unavailable", reason: e.message, attempts: 2, at: io.now() };
            throw e;
          }
        });
        budget.settle(0, r.attempts);
        stats.llmTurns++;
        lastAt = r.at;

        if (r.kind === "unavailable") {
          mode = "rule_based";
          break;
        }
        if (r.attempts === 2) fallbackTurns++;
        if (r.findingsArgs !== null) lastFindingsArgs = r.findingsArgs;
        if (r.findings) {
          findings = r.findings;
          fromModel = true;
          if (finishReason) limit ??= finishReason;
          break;
        }
        if (finishReason) {
          // The finish-only turn didn't produce usable findings: the rules fill in, keeping its valid fields.
          limit ??= finishReason;
          break;
        }
        if (r.invalid.length && ++invalidTurns >= 2) break;

        const notes = [...r.invalid];
        const calls = r.tools.slice(0, toolsLeft);
        if (r.tools.length > calls.length) notes.push(`Only ${toolsLeft} tool call(s) were left, so ${r.tools.length - calls.length} call(s) were dropped.`);
        feedback = notes.length ? notes.join("\n") : undefined;
        if (calls.length === 0) continue;

        const ids = await io.step(`pending-${turn}`, () => io.agent.addPendingChecks(runId, turn, calls.map(({ tool, args }) => ({ tool, args }))));
        budget.settle(0, 1);
        for (const [i, call] of calls.entries()) {
          if (lastAt >= deadline) {
            limit ??= "limit_deadline";
            break;
          }
          await runCheck(ids[i]!, call);
        }
        if (limit === "limit_deadline") break;
      }
    }

    if (!findings) {
      // Rule path / gap fill (TDD §8.2): the baseline checks this run hasn't done, if budget and time allow.
      await runBaseline(RULES_TURN, "pending-rules");
      const args = lastFindingsArgs;
      findings = await io.step("rules", () => io.agent.ruleFindings(runId, args));
    }

    const final = findings;
    await io.step("findings", () => io.agent.setFindings(runId, final, mode));
    // WP-13b: the card shows as soon as the findings exist; the narration streams while it waits.
    const proposal = await io.step("propose", () => io.agent.proposeAction(runId, io.workflowId));
    await io.step("narrate", () => io.agent.narrate(runId));
    const status = limit ?? (mode === "rule_based" && ctx.mode === "ai" ? "llm_unavailable" : "done");
    const source: FindingsSource = fromModel ? "model" : lastFindingsArgs !== null ? "model+rules" : "rules";
    await io.step("finish", () => io.agent.finishRun(runId, status, { ...stats, fallbackTurns, source }));
    return { summary: { status, mode, ...stats, category: final.category }, approvalId: proposal.approvalId };
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("diagnosis run failed", runId, (e as Error).message);
    try {
      await io.step("finish-failed", () => io.agent.finishRun(runId, "failed", stats));
    } catch {
      // Nothing more to do: the agent's activeRunId stays set until someone resolves the incident.
    }
    return { summary: { status: "failed", mode, ...stats }, approvalId: null };
  }
}

/**
 * The approval tail (TDD §8.2, FR-20 to FR-23): wait for the human, then run the action and verify it. Rejected
 * or expired cards are recorded, and nothing runs. A failure here never touches the finished run.
 */
async function approvalTail(io: RunIO, runId: string, approvalId: string): Promise<ActionSummary> {
  try {
    const decision = await io.waitForApproval();
    if (decision !== "approved") {
      await io.step(`approval-${decision}`, () => io.agent.recordApprovalOutcome(approvalId, decision));
      return { approvalId, decision };
    }
    let out: ActionOutcome;
    try {
      out = await io.step("action", () => io.agent.executeAction(approvalId));
    } catch (e) {
      out = { ok: false, text: (e as Error).message };
    }
    if (!out.ok) {
      const detail = out.text;
      await io.step("action-failed", () => io.agent.recordApprovalOutcome(approvalId, "failed", detail));
      return { approvalId, decision, verified: false };
    }
    const v = await io.step("verify", () => io.agent.afterAction(runId, approvalId));
    return { approvalId, decision, verified: v.pass };
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("approval tail failed", runId, approvalId, (e as Error).message);
    return { approvalId, decision: "error" };
  }
}

function errorResult(evidenceId: string, tool: ToolName, e: unknown): ToolResult {
  const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
  return { evidenceId, tool, ok: false, status: "error", summary: `Check failed: ${message.slice(0, 200)}`, digest: JSON.stringify({ error: message.slice(0, 200) }), raw: null };
}

function toLine(r: ToolResult): EvidenceLine {
  return { evidenceId: r.evidenceId, tool: r.tool, status: r.status, summary: r.summary, digest: r.digest };
}
```

```
import { z } from "zod";
import { BudgetExceededError, HIGH_LATENCY_TOTAL_MS, HIGH_LATENCY_TTFB_MS, HTTP_SAMPLES, SNIPPET_TO_LLM } from "../loop/budget";
import { followHops, readOnce, type HopResult, type HopStop, type Method } from "../safety/reader";
import { createGuard } from "../safety/ssrf";
import { fitDigest, untrusted } from "./digest";
import type { CheckStatus, ToolContext, ToolResult } from "./types";

/** 2 guard DoH lookups + 3 samples × (1 + 5 hops). The asset sub-probe only runs if room is left. */
export const HTTP_PROBE_CAP = 20;
const RAW_SNIPPET = 2048;
const PICKED_HEADERS = ["server", "cf-ray", "cf-cache-status", "cache-control", "content-type", "location"] as const;
const TEXTUAL = /^(text\/|application\/(json|xml|xhtml\+xml|javascript))/i;
/** Locally, workerd hides the reason ("internal error; reference = …"), so this only matches clearer runtime messages. */
const TLS_ERROR = /\b(tls|ssl|certificate|handshake)\b/i;

export const httpInput = z.object({
  url: z.string().refine((u) => {
    try {
      return ["http:", "https:"].includes(new URL(u).protocol);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }, "must be an http or https URL"),
  method: z.enum(["GET", "HEAD"]).default("GET"),
});
export type HttpInput = z.output<typeof httpInput>;

export type Sample = {
  status: number | null;
  ttfbMs: number | null;
  totalMs: number;
  stop: HopStop;
  error?: string;
  /** The runtime's error text for a failed request, at most 200 chars. */
  message?: string;
  hops: { url: string; status: number; location: string | null }[];
};
export type AssetProbe =
  | { url: string; status: number; cacheControl: string | null; setCookie: boolean }
  | { url: string; error: string };

export type HttpRaw = {
  url: string;
  method: Method;
  samples: Sample[];
  medianTotalMs: number | null;
  medianTtfbMs: number | null;
  hops: Sample["hops"];
  headers: Partial<Record<(typeof PICKED_HEADERS)[number], string>> & { setCookie: boolean };
  snippet: string | null;
  asset: AssetProbe | null;
  refused?: { code: string; message: string };
  partial?: "rate_limited" | "budget";
};

export async function runHttpProbe(input: HttpInput, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult<HttpRaw>> {
  const now = ctx.now ?? (() => Date.now());
  const guard = createGuard({ fetch: ctx.fetch, allowOrigin: ctx.allowOrigin });
  const host = new URL(input.url).hostname;
  const samples: Sample[] = [];
  let first: HopResult | null = null;
  let partial: HttpRaw["partial"];

  for (let i = 0; i < HTTP_SAMPLES; i++) {
    if (ctx.rateLimit && !(await ctx.rateLimit(host))) {
      partial = "rate_limited";
      break;
    }
    let result: HopResult;
    try {
      result = await followHops(ctx.fetch, guard, input.url, input.method, { now });
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof BudgetExceededError || (e as Error).name === "BudgetExceededError") {
        partial = "budget";
        break;
      }
      throw e;
    }
    first ??= result;
    samples.push(toSample(result));
    // Loops, hop caps and refusals are deterministic: more samples add cost, not evidence.
    if (result.stop === "loop" || result.stop === "max_hops" || result.stop === "blocked") break;
  }

  const withResponse = samples.filter((s) => s.status !== null);
  const finalRes = first?.final ?? null;
  const headers: HttpRaw["headers"] = { setCookie: false };
  if (finalRes) {
    for (const h of PICKED_HEADERS) {
      const v = finalRes.headers.get(h);
      if (v !== null) headers[h] = v.slice(0, 200);
    }
    headers.setCookie = finalRes.headers.has("set-cookie");
  }
  const contentType = finalRes?.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  const textual = finalRes !== null && TEXTUAL.test(contentType) && finalRes.body.length > 0;

  let asset: AssetProbe | null = null;
  if (finalRes && first?.stop === "done" && input.method === "GET" && /html/i.test(contentType)) {
    const assetUrl = findAsset(finalRes.body, finalRes.url);
    if (assetUrl) asset = await probeAsset(ctx, guard, assetUrl, now);
  }

  const raw: HttpRaw = {
    url: input.url,
    method: input.method,
    samples,
    medianTotalMs: median(withResponse.map((s) => s.totalMs)),
    medianTtfbMs: median(withResponse.map((s) => s.ttfbMs ?? 0)),
    hops: samples[0]?.hops ?? [],
    headers,
    snippet: textual ? finalRes.body.slice(0, RAW_SNIPPET) : null,
    asset,
  };
  if (first?.stop === "blocked" && first.blocked) raw.refused = { code: first.blocked.code, message: first.blocked.message };
  if (partial) raw.partial = partial;

  const { status, summary } = judge(raw);
  return {
    evidenceId: ctx.evidenceId,
    tool: "http_probe",
    ok: status === "pass" || status === "fail",
    status,
    summary,
    digest: httpDigest(raw, textual && finalRes ? untrusted(finalRes.body, SNIPPET_TO_LLM) : null),
    raw,
  };
}

function toSample(r: HopResult): Sample {
  const hops = r.hops.map((h) => ({ url: h.url, status: h.status, location: h.location }));
  const totalMs = r.hops.reduce((sum, h) => sum + h.totalMs, 0) + (r.error && "totalMs" in r.error ? r.error.totalMs : 0);
  const last = r.hops.at(-1);
  const sample: Sample = {
    status: r.stop === "error" || r.stop === "blocked" ? null : (last?.status ?? null),
    ttfbMs: r.stop === "error" || r.stop === "blocked" ? null : (last?.ttfbMs ?? null),
    totalMs,
    stop: r.stop,
    hops,
  };
import { z } from "zod";
import { dohQuery, RESOLVERS, RR_TYPES, type RrType } from "../lib/doh";
import { isBlockedIp } from "../safety/iprange";
import { fitDigest } from "./digest";
import type { CheckStatus, ToolContext, ToolResult } from "./types";

export const DNS_TYPES = ["A", "AAAA", "CNAME", "NS", "MX", "TXT"] as const satisfies readonly RrType[];
const HOSTNAME = /^(?=.{1,253}\.?$)(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z]{2,63}\.?$/i;

export const dnsInput = z.object({
  hostname: z
    .string()
    .regex(HOSTNAME, "must be a public hostname, not an IP address or URL")
    .describe("A public hostname like shop.example.com: no scheme, path or IP address.")
    .transform((h) => h.toLowerCase().replace(/\.$/, "")),
  types: z.array(z.enum(DNS_TYPES)).min(1).max(DNS_TYPES.length).default(["A", "AAAA"]),
});
export type DnsInput = z.output<typeof dnsInput>;

const RCODES: Record<number, string> = { 0: "NOERROR", 1: "FORMERR", 2: "SERVFAIL", 3: "NXDOMAIN", 4: "NOTIMP", 5: "REFUSED" };
const TYPE_NAMES = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(RR_TYPES).map(([k, v]) => [v, k])) as Record<number, string>;
const ADDRESS_TYPES = new Set<number>([RR_TYPES.A, RR_TYPES.AAAA]);
type ResolverName = keyof typeof RESOLVERS;
const RESOLVER_NAMES = Object.keys(RESOLVERS) as ResolverName[];

export type DnsAnswer = { type: string; data: string; ttl: number };
export type TypeResult = { status: string; answers: DnsAnswer[] } | { status: "ERROR"; error: string; answers: [] };

export type DnsRaw = {
  hostname: string;
  types: string[];
  resolvers: Record<ResolverName, Record<string, TypeResult>>;
  addresses: Record<ResolverName, string[]>;
  cnameChain: string[];
  /** CNAME targets the origin pin allows for dns_lookup/cert_info (D6). */
  cnameTargets: string[];
  /** Resolvers disagree on the response code or on whether the name has any address. */
  mismatch: boolean;
  /** Both have addresses but different sets; normal for geo/CDN DNS, informational only. */
  addressesDiffer: boolean;
  ssrfBlocked: boolean;
};

const stripDot = (s: string) => s.replace(/\.$/, "");

export async function runDnsLookup(input: DnsInput, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult<DnsRaw>> {
  const jobs = RESOLVER_NAMES.flatMap((resolver) =>
    input.types.map(async (type) => {
      try {
        const r = await dohQuery(ctx.fetch, RESOLVERS[resolver], input.hostname, type);
        const result: TypeResult = {
          status: RCODES[r.status] ?? `RCODE${r.status}`,
          answers: r.answers.map((a) => ({ type: TYPE_NAMES[a.type] ?? String(a.type), data: stripDot(a.data), ttl: a.ttl })),
        };
        return { resolver, type, result };
      } catch (e) {
        if ((e as Error).name === "BudgetExceededError") throw e;
        const result: TypeResult = { status: "ERROR", error: (e as Error).message, answers: [] };
        return { resolver, type, result };
      }
    }),
  );
  const settled = await Promise.all(jobs);

  const resolvers = { cloudflare: {}, google: {} } as DnsRaw["resolvers"];
  for (const { resolver, type, result } of settled) resolvers[resolver][type] = result;

  const addresses = Object.fromEntries(
    RESOLVER_NAMES.map((r) => [
      r,
      [...new Set(Object.values(resolvers[r]).flatMap((t) => t.answers.filter((a) => ADDRESS_TYPES.has(RR_TYPES[a.type as RrType])).map((a) => a.data)))],
    ]),
  ) as Record<ResolverName, string[]>;

  const cnameChain = [
    ...new Set(
      RESOLVER_NAMES.flatMap((r) => Object.values(resolvers[r]).flatMap((t) => t.answers.filter((a) => a.type === "CNAME").map((a) => a.data))),
    ),
  ];
```

```
import type { IncidentStatus } from "../agent/state";
import { ACTION_IDS, type ActionId, type Category, type Findings } from "../diagnosis/categories";
import { LIMITS } from "../lib/limits";
import { createWatch, listWatches } from "../watch/store";

/**
 * Action catalog (TDD §7.6). The model and the rules only *name* actions; this decides which one is shown as
 * an approval card, and nothing runs until a human approves it (FR-20, FR-24).
 */
export const ACTION_TITLES: Record<ActionId, string> = {
  restore_demo_service: "Restore the demo service to healthy",
  add_to_watchlist: "Add this endpoint to the watchlist",
  recheck_burst: "Re-check every minute for 15 minutes",
  generate_postmortem: "Write a postmortem",
};

export type ProposeContext = {
  target: string;
  workspaceId: string;
  demoBaseUrl: string;
  category: Category;
  status: IncidentStatus;
  /** The incident isn't watched yet, its URL isn't on the workspace's watchlist, and the caps allow one more. */
  watchable: boolean;
};

/** True when the target is this workspace's own demo page: someone else's demo, or any other site, is off limits. */
export function isOwnDemo(target: string, workspaceId: string, demoBaseUrl: string): boolean {
  if (!URL.canParse(target) || !URL.canParse(demoBaseUrl)) return false;
  const t = new URL(target);
  return t.origin === new URL(demoBaseUrl).origin && t.pathname.startsWith(`/w/${workspaceId}/`);
}

export function proposable(action: ActionId, ctx: ProposeContext): boolean {
  // A new card only fits these statuses (TDD §10): mitigated and resolved have no edge to awaiting_approval.
  if (ctx.status !== "investigating" && ctx.status !== "awaiting_approval") return false;
  switch (action) {
    case "restore_demo_service":
      return ctx.category !== "healthy" && isOwnDemo(ctx.target, ctx.workspaceId, ctx.demoBaseUrl);
    case "add_to_watchlist":
      // A real site that's broken: watching it tells the user when it recovers. The demo gets a restore instead.
      return ctx.category !== "healthy" && ctx.watchable && !isOwnDemo(ctx.target, ctx.workspaceId, ctx.demoBaseUrl);
    // Switched on by their WPs: generate_postmortem (WP-22), recheck_burst (WP-24).
    case "recheck_burst":
    case "generate_postmortem":
      return false;
  }
}

/**
 * One action per run (D13). A restore that fits always wins, since a fix beats watching (WP-18). Otherwise the
 * model's actions come first, then the rule actions, so a broken demo always gets "Restore demo service" and a
 * broken real site "Add to watchlist" even when the model leaves them out (user decisions, WP-12 and WP-18).
 */
export function pickAction(findings: Pick<Findings, "category" | "actions">, ctx: ProposeContext): ActionId | null {
  if (proposable("restore_demo_service", ctx)) return "restore_demo_service";
  const rules: ActionId[] = findings.category === "healthy" ? [] : ["add_to_watchlist"];
  const candidates = [...new Set([...findings.actions, ...rules])].filter((a): a is ActionId => (ACTION_IDS as readonly string[]).includes(a));
  return candidates.find((a) => proposable(a, ctx)) ?? null;
}

/** `watchId`: the watch an `add_to_watchlist` created or found, for the incident to link to. */
export type ActionOutcome = { ok: boolean; text: string; watchId?: string };

export type ExecuteContext = { env: Pick<Env, "DEMO" | "DB">; workspaceId: string; incidentId: string; target: string };

export const WATCH_ADDED_TEXT = `Added to the watchlist: checked every ${LIMITS.watchMinIntervalSec / 60} minutes for ${LIMITS.watchTtlHours} hours. This incident resolves itself after 3 passing checks in a row.`;

/** Runs an approved action. Idempotent: a retried Workflow step may call it again. */
export async function execute(action: ActionId, ctx: ExecuteContext): Promise<ActionOutcome> {
  switch (action) {
    case "restore_demo_service":
      await ctx.env.DEMO.setModes(ctx.workspaceId, ["healthy"]);
      return { ok: true, text: "The demo service is back in healthy mode." };
    case "add_to_watchlist": {
      const r = await createWatch(ctx.env.DB, { workspaceId: ctx.workspaceId, url: ctx.target, intervalSec: LIMITS.watchMinIntervalSec, openIncidentId: ctx.incidentId });
      if (r.ok) return { ok: true, text: WATCH_ADDED_TEXT, watchId: r.watch.id };
      // A retried step finds its own watch.
      const mine = (await listWatches(ctx.env.DB, ctx.workspaceId)).find((w) => w.url === ctx.target && w.open_incident_id === ctx.incidentId);
      if (mine) return { ok: true, text: WATCH_ADDED_TEXT, watchId: mine.id };
      const why = { already_watched: "this URL is already on the watchlist", watch_limit: "the watchlist is full (3 per workspace)", watch_capacity: "the app is at its limit of 30 watches" }[r.error];
      return { ok: false, text: `Couldn't add the watch: ${why}.` };
    }
    default:
      return { ok: false, text: `${ACTION_TITLES[action]} isn't available yet.` };
  }
}
import type { Category, Confidence, Findings } from "../diagnosis/categories";
import type { CheckStatus, ToolName } from "../tools/types";

/** JSON values: anything stored in state or returned over RPC must be serialisable. */
export type Json = string | number | boolean | null | Json[] | { [key: string]: Json };
/** Timeline details are flat on purpose: a recursive type in the state makes RPC stub types too deep. */
export type Scalar = string | number | boolean | null;

/** Synced state (TDD §6.3): small on purpose; full evidence goes through `getEvidence`. */
export type IncidentStatus = "investigating" | "awaiting_approval" | "mitigated" | "resolved";
export type Origin = "chat" | "watch";
export type RunMode = "ai" | "rule_based";
export type RunKind = "initial" | "followup";
/** Why a run ended (TDD §8.2 "Limits keep progress"); `running` while it's active. */
export const RUN_STATUSES = ["running", "done", "limit_turns", "limit_tools", "limit_deadline", "limit_budget", "llm_unavailable", "failed"] as const;
export type RunStatus = (typeof RUN_STATUSES)[number];

export type CheckView = {
  evidenceId: string;
  runId: string;
  tool: ToolName;
  label: string;
  status: "pending" | "running" | CheckStatus;
  summary: string;
  startedAt: number;
  finishedAt?: number;
};
export type MessageView = { id: string; runId: string | null; role: "user" | "assistant" | "system"; content: string; createdAt: number };
export type ApprovalStatus = "pending" | "approved" | "rejected" | "expired" | "superseded";
/** An approval card (TDD §12.2). `cause`/`evidence` come from the proposing run's findings, so an old card keeps its own "why". */
export type ApprovalView = {
  id: string;
  runId: string;
  actionId: string;
  title: string;
  status: ApprovalStatus;
  cause: string;
  evidence: string[];
  /** What happened after the decision. `verified` is set once the re-check after the action has run. */
  /** `verified`: the re-check after a fix. `done`: an action with nothing to re-check (add_to_watchlist). */
  outcome?: { ok: boolean; text: string; verified?: boolean; done?: boolean };
  createdAt: number;
  decidedAt?: number;
};
export type TimelineView = { id: number; ts: number; type: string; detail: Record<string, Scalar> };

export type IncidentState = {
  id: string;
  target: string;
  origin: Origin;
  status: IncidentStatus;
  category: Category | null;
  confidence: Confidence | null;
  /** Mode of the latest run. */
  mode: RunMode;
  /** Non-null → the composer is disabled (D13). */
  activeRunId: string | null;
  /** Latest run's findings (for the approval card's "why"). */
  findings: Findings | null;
  checks: CheckView[];
  messages: MessageView[];
  approvals: ApprovalView[];
  timeline: TimelineView[];
  monitor: { kind: "post_action" | "burst"; passes: number; ticksLeft: number } | null;
  hasPostmortem: boolean;
};

/** WebSocket side channel (broadcast, not state). `stream_start` resets the buffer when a retried narration restreams. */
export type StreamFrame = { type: "stream_start"; messageId: string } | { type: "delta"; messageId: string; text: string } | { type: "stream_end"; messageId: string };

export type ConnectionRole = "owner" | "viewer";
/** `ip` is the visitor's hashed `ipKey` (safety/quota.ts) for the per-IP run quota; null without an address. */
export type ConnectionInfo = { role: ConnectionRole; ip: string | null };

export const STATE_CAPS = { checks: 40, messages: 50, approvals: 5, timeline: 100 } as const;

export const EMPTY_STATE: IncidentState = {
  id: "",
  target: "",
  origin: "chat",
  status: "investigating",
  category: null,
  confidence: null,
  mode: "ai",
  activeRunId: null,
  findings: null,
  checks: [],
  messages: [],
  approvals: [],
  timeline: [],
  monitor: null,
  hasPostmortem: false,
};
/**
 * IncidentAgent SQLite (TDD §6.2), created in `onStart`. Additions to the TDD sketch:
 * `incident.watch_id`/`unread`, `runs.question`/`mode`, and `checks.turn`/`seq` with a unique key so
 * `addPendingChecks` returns the same evidence IDs when a Workflow step retries.
 */
export const SCHEMA = [
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS incident (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id TEXT NOT NULL, target TEXT NOT NULL,
    origin TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, category TEXT, confidence TEXT, mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ai',
    watch_id TEXT, unread INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL, updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, run_id TEXT, role TEXT NOT NULL, content TEXT NOT NULL,
    evidence_refs_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]', created_at INTEGER NOT NULL)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runs (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, seq INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE, kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    question TEXT NOT NULL, workflow_id TEXT, status TEXT NOT NULL, mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ai',
    started_at INTEGER NOT NULL, finished_at INTEGER, findings_json TEXT, tool_calls INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    llm_turns INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS checks (evidence_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, run_id TEXT NOT NULL, turn INTEGER NOT NULL,
    seq INTEGER NOT NULL, tool TEXT NOT NULL, args_json TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, summary TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    digest TEXT, raw_json TEXT, started_at INTEGER NOT NULL, finished_at INTEGER, UNIQUE (run_id, turn, seq))`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS approvals (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, run_id TEXT NOT NULL, action_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    args_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', status TEXT NOT NULL, workflow_id TEXT, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
    decided_at INTEGER, outcome_json TEXT)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS timeline (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, ts INTEGER NOT NULL,
    event_type TEXT NOT NULL, detail_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}')`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS monitor (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1), kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    ticks_left INTEGER NOT NULL, consecutive_passes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, schedule_id TEXT)`,
  `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS postmortem (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1), markdown TEXT NOT NULL,
    generated_at INTEGER NOT NULL)`,
] as const;
import { LIMITS } from "../lib/limits";
import { devAllowOrigin } from "./ssrf";

/** `retryAfter` is in seconds, for the 429 body, the `Retry-After` header and the UI's "try again in …". */
export type Quota = { ok: true } | { ok: false; retryAfter: number };

const MINUTE_MS = 60_000;
const HOUR_MS = 3_600_000;

/**
 * Fixed-window counter in D1 `usage_counters` (TDD §11). One atomic upsert per call, so two requests can't
 * both read the old count. Every call counts, refused ones too; the window still ends on time.
 */
export async function takeWindow(db: D1Database, workspaceId: string, kind: "run" | "voice" | "webhook_test", limit: number, windowMs: number, now: number): Promise<Quota> {
  const windowStart = Math.floor(now / windowMs) * windowMs;
  const row = await db
    .prepare(
      `INSERT INTO usage_counters (workspace_id, kind, window_start, count) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, 1)
       ON CONFLICT (workspace_id, kind, window_start) DO UPDATE SET count = count + 1 RETURNING count`,
    )
    .bind(workspaceId, kind, windowStart)
    .first<{ count: number }>();
  if ((row?.count ?? 0) <= limit) return { ok: true };
  return { ok: false, retryAfter: Math.max(1, Math.ceil((windowStart + windowMs - now) / 1000)) };
}

/** Who is asking: the workspace, and the visitor's IP as an `ipKey` (null when the request carries none). */
export type Caller = { workspaceId: string; ip: string | null };

/**
 * The D1 and rate-limit key for a visitor IP: `ip:` plus a SHA-256 prefix, so raw addresses aren't stored.
 * Null without an address (local tools and tests that don't send `CF-Connecting-IP`; Cloudflare always sets it).
 */
export async function ipKey(ip: string | null | undefined): Promise<string | null> {
  if (!ip) return null;
  const digest = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(ip)));
  return `ip:${[...digest.slice(0, 16)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("")}`;
}

/** One Rate Limiting binding call; an error is logged and allows the run (fail open). */
async function burst(binding: RateLimit, key: string, name: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    return (await binding.limit({ key })).success;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(`${name} failed; allowing the run`, (e as Error).message);
    return true;
  }
}

/** One D1 window; an error is logged and allows the run (fail open). */
async function hourly(db: D1Database, key: string, limit: number, now: number): Promise<Quota> {
  try {
    return await takeWindow(db, key, "run", limit, HOUR_MS, now);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("run quota (D1) failed; allowing the run", (e as Error).message);
    return { ok: true };
  }
}

/**
 * The single "LLM run" quota (D14): new incidents and follow-ups. Per workspace, 3 a minute (RUN_LIMIT) and
 * 10 an hour (D1). Per visitor IP as well, 6 a minute (RUN_IP_LIMIT) and 20 an hour (D1, keyed by `ipKey`),
 * because a client that drops the cookie gets a new workspace each time. Fails open: a binding or D1 error
 * is logged and the run goes ahead, since the budget guard and the Workers AI allocation still protect neurons.
 */
export async function takeRun(env: Pick<Env, "RUN_LIMIT" | "RUN_IP_LIMIT" | "DB">, caller: Caller, now: number = Date.now()): Promise<Quota> {
  // The binding doesn't say when its window resets, so the burst answer is the worst case: a minute.
  const minute: Quota = { ok: false, retryAfter: MINUTE_MS / 1000 };
  if (!(await burst(env.RUN_LIMIT, caller.workspaceId, "RUN_LIMIT"))) return minute;
  if (caller.ip && !(await burst(env.RUN_IP_LIMIT, caller.ip, "RUN_IP_LIMIT"))) return minute;
  const ws = await hourly(env.DB, caller.workspaceId, LIMITS.runsPerHour, now);
  if (!ws.ok) return ws;
  return caller.ip ? hourly(env.DB, caller.ip, LIMITS.runsPerHourPerIp, now) : ws;
}

/**
 * `ToolContext.rateLimit` backed by PROBE_LIMIT, keyed by hostname (TDD §11). Our own demo Worker (and the
 * dev allow-origin) isn't limited: every workspace's demo shares one hostname, and the limit exists to protect
 * other people's sites. Fails open, like the run quota.
 */
export function probeLimiter(env: Pick<Env, "PROBE_LIMIT" | "DEMO_BASE_URL">): (host: string) => Promise<boolean> {
  const exempt = new Set([env.DEMO_BASE_URL, devAllowOrigin(env)].flatMap((u) => (u && URL.canParse(u) ? [new URL(u).hostname] : [])));
  return async (host) => {
    if (exempt.has(host)) return true;
    try {
      return (await env.PROBE_LIMIT.limit({ key: host })).success;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("PROBE_LIMIT failed; allowing the probe", (e as Error).message);
      return true;
    }
  };
}

api.route("/incidents", incidents);
api.route("/demo", demo);
api.route("/watches", watches);
```
