/**
 * Deterministic target parser for the first chat message (D9). Order: URL, then host token, then the
 * word "demo". No safety checks here: private hosts are extracted on purpose so the SSRF guard (WP-04)
 * can refuse them with a clear message.
 */
export type TargetResult =
  | { ok: true; url: string; host: string; source: "url" | "host" | "demo" }
  | { ok: false; error: "need_target" };

const MAX_SCAN = 2000;
const SPOKEN_DOT = /([a-z0-9-])\s+dot\s+(?=[a-z0-9])/gi;
const URL_TOKEN = /\bhttps?:\/\/[^\s<>"'`]+/i;
const TRAILING_PUNCT = /[.,;:!?)\]}'"`]+$/;
// IPv4 literal | localhost | hostname with an alphabetic TLD.
const HOST_TOKEN =
  /\b((?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}|localhost|(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z]{2,63})\.?(?![a-z0-9-])/gi;
const FILE_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
  "html", "htm", "js", "mjs", "cjs", "json", "css", "php", "txt", "md", "ts", "tsx", "jsx",
  "png", "jpg", "jpeg", "gif", "svg", "ico", "yaml", "yml", "log", "xml", "csv", "sh", "py",
]);
const DEMO_WORD = /\bdemo\b/i;

export function parseTarget(message: string, demoUrl: string): TargetResult {
  const text = message.slice(0, MAX_SCAN).replace(SPOKEN_DOT, "$1.");

  const url = fromUrlToken(text);
  if (url) return url;

  const host = fromHostToken(text);
  if (host) return host;

  if (DEMO_WORD.test(text)) {
    return { ok: true, url: demoUrl, host: new URL(demoUrl).hostname, source: "demo" };
  }
  return { ok: false, error: "need_target" };
}

function fromUrlToken(text: string): TargetResult | null {
  const match = URL_TOKEN.exec(text);
  if (!match) return null;
  try {
    const u = new URL(match[0].replace(TRAILING_PUNCT, ""));
    u.username = "";
    u.password = "";
    u.hash = "";
    return { ok: true, url: u.toString(), host: u.hostname, source: "url" };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function fromHostToken(text: string): TargetResult | null {
  for (const match of text.matchAll(HOST_TOKEN)) {
    const host = match[1]!.toLowerCase();
    if (isIpv4Shaped(host)) {
      if (!host.split(".").every((o) => Number(o) <= 255)) continue;
    } else if (host !== "localhost" && FILE_EXTENSIONS.has(host.slice(host.lastIndexOf(".") + 1))) {
      continue;
    }
    return { ok: true, url: `https://${host}/`, host, source: "host" };
  }
  return null;
}

function isIpv4Shaped(host: string): boolean {
  return /^(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}$/.test(host);
}

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: WP-21 (cert_info + provider_status), WP-22 (Postmortem), WP-23 (Voice)

## Context

Everything above the cut line (WP-01 to WP-20, plus WP-27/27b/27c) is done. These are the next three items below the cut line, in priority order. They are listed in the README's "Designed, not built" table, the TDD stubs exist (`ToolName` already includes `cert_info`/`provider_status`, `CertRaw`/`ProviderRaw` types, the classify rules 2 and 5, the `postmortem` SQL table, `hasPostmortem` state, the `generate_postmortem` action ID, `VOICE_LIMIT` binding, `usage_counters` kind `voice`, `LIMITS.voicePerHour`), and nothing calls them yet.

**User decisions (this session):**
1. Cert and provider checks run on their own **only when useful**: cert when HTTPS requests fail to connect, provider status when the site is failing and is fingerprinted to Cloudflare / GitHub Pages / AWS. The model can still call either anytime.
2. Postmortem: **facts by code, story by AI**. Impact window, detection and timeline come from the incident's records; the AI writes summary, root cause, resolution, follow-ups. Template when AI is off.
3. Voice: **click to start, click to stop**, auto-stop at 30 s, text fills the input and is never sent.

Build order 21 → 22 → 23, one commit per WP on `dev` (pushed to `dev`, never to `main`). After each WP: `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test` (3 green runs in a row, as before), TASKS.md board + decisions log, PROMPTS.md, README "Designed, not built" table, TDD fold-ins.

No new bindings and no D1 migration are needed.

---

## WP-21: `cert_info` + `provider_status`

### 0. Spike (about 15 min, throwaway `/api/dev/*` route, removed before commit, as in WP-05)
From workerd (`pnpm dev`), with real network:
- crt.sh JSON for `cloudflare.com`, the deployed demo host (`*.workers.dev`: are wildcard certs found by an exact-host query?), `expired.badssl.com`; check field names (`issuer_name`, `common_name`, `name_value`, `not_before`, `not_after`), sort order, timing.
- `www.cloudflarestatus.com/api/v2/incidents/unresolved.json`, `www.githubstatus.com/...` shape; one AWS public health feed (best effort).
- Whether `caches.default` works under `vite dev`.
Findings go in the TASKS decisions log, then into TDD §7.3.

### 1. `worker/tools/cert.ts` (new)
- Input `{hostname}`, reusing the hostname schema from `tools/dns.ts` (`dnsInput.shape.hostname`).
- Fetch `https://crt.sh/?q=<host>&output=json&exclude=expired&deduplicate=Y` with `readOnce` (`safety/reader.ts`) at `readCap` 256 KB (`CRT_READ_CAP`), timeout about 8 s. A truncated body is parsed leniently (cut at the last complete object). Parse at most 20 entries (`CRT_MAX_ENTRIES`).
- `sanMatch`: exact name or a one-label wildcard in `name_value`/`common_name`. Keep the 3 matching certs with the most days left.
- **Expired detection:** `exclude=expired` hides expired certs, so when no unexpired cert matches, one more query without `exclude` looks for the latest expired match (cap = 2 fetches; TDD said 1, logged as a deviation). If the spike shows exact queries miss wildcard certs, the second query uses `*.parent` instead.
- Cache API: `caches.default`, key = crt.sh URL, 1 h; every cache call in try/catch (a no-op cache must not break the tool). Check on the deployed workers.dev site after the next deploy and note the result.
- Results: `unavailable` on timeout, network error, non-200 or unparsable JSON ("Certificate info unavailable: crt.sh didn't answer", never a failure signal, PRD R4); `unavailable` when no matching cert is found; `fail` when the best cert is expired or expires within 7 days; `pass` otherwise. Summary always ends "(CT logs, best effort)". Raw = `CertRaw` (+ `query`). Digest via `fitDigest` (`tools/digest.ts`).

### 2. `worker/tools/provider.ts` (new)
- Input `{providers: ("cloudflare"|"github"|"aws")[]}` (1–3, deduped), using `PROVIDERS` from `diagnosis/categories.ts`.
- Statuspage `unresolved.json` for Cloudflare and GitHub (fixed `www.` URLs, 64 KB cap, `readOnce`, in parallel); AWS from the spike's feed, best effort. Fixed hosts, so no SSRF guard is needed (D6 fixed list).
- Raw = `ProviderRaw` plus `unavailable: Provider[]`. Status: `fail` if any provider has an active incident, `pass` if none, `unavailable` if every provider failed. Cap = number of providers.

### 3. Registry, prompt, origin pin
- `worker/tools/registry.ts`: register both in `TOOLS` (labels "Certificate", "Provider status"; model-facing descriptions saying cert is CT logs, best effort). Drop the "not registered until WP-21" comment.
- `worker/llm/prompts.ts` `planningSystem`: say `cert_info` takes the host or CNAME targets, `provider_status` takes providers only. `safety/origin.ts` already handles both.

### 4. "Only when useful" automatic checks (`worker/loop/extras.ts`, new, pure)
- The tool step in `loop/plan.ts` `runCheck` already returns `cnameTargets`; it also returns small **signals** computed from `raw` inside the step (so replays match): for `http_probe` `{https, connectFailed, failing, providers}`, using a shared `fingerprint()` moved from `diagnosis/classify.ts` into `diagnosis/fingerprint.ts` (classify imports it back).
- `extraCalls(signals, ranThisRun)` → `cert_info {hostname}` if the target is https and a sample failed with no response; `provider_status {providers}` if HTTP is failing and a provider is fingerprinted. Unit-tested.
- Run them right after the baseline (new step `pending-extras`, turn index 98) so the model sees them, and in the rule path after `pending-rules` (turn index 100). Same budget/deadline guards as `runBaseline`; they count against the 6-tool cap.

### 5. Rules (`worker/diagnosis/classify.ts`)
- Rule 2: an expired best cert counts only when HTTP isn't fully healthy (guards against CT search gaps). Unchanged otherwise. Rule 5 already reads `ProviderRaw`.

### 6. Tests
- Unit: `cert.test.ts` (parse, truncated JSON, wildcard SAN, days, expired fallback query, cache hit skips fetch, 502/timeout/bad JSON → `unavailable`), `provider.test.ts` (Statuspage parse, AWS down but others counted, all down → `unavailable`), `extras.test.ts`, more `classify.test.ts` fixtures (expired, ≤ 7 days, unavailable cert ignored, provider outage), `registry.test.ts`/`prompts.test.ts` updates. Fakes via `test/unit/helpers/fake-fetch.ts`.
- Integration: extend the `fake-internet` worker in `vitest.config.ts` with `crt.sh` and the status hosts (plus a host whose crt.sh lookup fails); `diagnosis.test.ts`: a connect-failing https host gets cert + status cards; crt failure → `unavailable`; existing tool-count asserts still hold.
- **Done when (local browser, mock LLM, real network):** `expired.badssl.com` shows a Certificate card and `tls_expiry`; with crt.sh unreachable the card shows Unavailable. The CheckCard already has icons for both tools.

---

## WP-22: Postmortem

### 1. `worker/agent/postmortem.ts` (new, pure)
- `postmortemFacts(...)` from the incident row, the **full** timeline (SQL, not the 100-row state), runs, checks and approvals: impact window (opened → resolved, with duration), detection (chat question or "Scheduled check failed for <url>"), timeline lines in UTC (opened, findings, approval decisions, action, verify, status changes; monitor/watch ticks collapsed into one line with a count).
- `renderPostmortem(facts, prose)` in fixed order: `# Postmortem: <host>`, Summary, Impact window, Detection, Timeline, Root cause (with `[ids]`), Resolution, Follow-ups.
- `parseProse(text, knownIds)`: splits the AI's `## Summary / Root cause / Resolution / Follow-ups` sections; `【id】` normalized and unknown IDs dropped (reuse helpers from `llm/citations.ts`); missing sections come from `templateProse(findings, approvals)`.

### 2. Agent (`worker/agent/IncidentAgent.ts`)
- `generatePostmortem()` callable: owner only; status must be `resolved` (`not_resolved`); an existing postmortem is returned without cost; otherwise `takeRun` (D14, IP from the connection, like `sendMessage`), then `budgetMode` → template, else `streamLlm` with frames under messageId `postmortem` (same `StreamFrame`s as narration), errors → template. An in-flight promise stops a double click from paying twice. Stores into `postmortem`, timeline event `postmortem`, `syncState` (`hasPostmortem`).
- `getPostmortem()` callable, open to viewers (read-only). Add both to the callable list at the end of the file.
- Prompts in `worker/llm/prompts.ts` (`postmortemSystem`, `postmortemUser`, given the facts + findings + digests). `worker/llm/mock.ts` answers postmortem prompts with canned sections.

### 3. `generate_postmortem` action (`worker/actions/catalog.ts`)
- `proposable`: status `resolved` and no postmortem yet (`hasPostmortem` added to `ProposeContext`); an exception to the investigating/awaiting rule. Model-named only, not a rule action.
- `executeAction` writes it through the shared internal writer (no quota: the card was approved); `afterAction` takes the existing "done, nothing to re-check" path used by `add_to_watchlist`. `proposeAction` already skips the status change outside `investigating`.

### 4. UI
- `web/components/PostmortemDialog.tsx` (Kumo `Dialog`, props from `node_modules/@cloudflare/kumo/ai/USAGE.md`): `DashedLoader` + live stream text while generating, then the stored markdown in `CodeBlock` (wraps, capped height like evidence), a Copy button with "Copied" feedback, inline error text (rate limit via `parseRateLimited`).
- Incident header (`IncidentView.tsx`): "Postmortem" button next to Resolve. Owner: shown always, disabled until resolved with the reason in words; opens the stored one if it exists. Viewer: "View postmortem" only once one exists.
- `useIncident`: add `generatePostmortem`, `getPostmortem`, and expose `streams`.

### 5. Tests
- Unit `postmortem.test.ts`: facts from a timeline fixture, impact window, collapsed ticks, prose parse (missing section → template, unknown IDs removed), template-only output.
- Integration `postmortem.test.ts`: not resolved → error; viewer forbidden; generate → stored + `hasPostmortem`; second call returns the same text without a quota charge; viewer can read; a `generate_postmortem` card on a resolved incident → approve → stored.
- **Done when:** local walkthrough (PRD §13) break → approve → resolved → Postmortem → markdown shown and copied; reload → still there.

---

## WP-23: Voice

### 1. Server
- `worker/voice/normalize.ts` (pure): " dot " → ".", " slash " → "/", hostname-looking tokens lowercased, trailing punctuation stripped from hostnames. `parseTarget` already handles spoken "dot", so this is for what the user sees.
- `worker/api/voice.ts`, mounted in `api/router.ts`: `audio/*` only (415); `Content-Length` and the real body ≤ 160 KB (`VOICE_MAX_BYTES`, 413 `too_large`); empty → 400; budget guard `rule_based` → 503 `voice_unavailable`; `VOICE_LIMIT` per workspace (5/min) then `takeWindow(..., "voice", LIMITS.voicePerHour, 1 h)` → 429 `{retryAfter}` + `Retry-After` (fail open, like `takeRun`); `LLM_MODE=mock` → canned "is demo dot example dot com down"; live → `env.AI.run("@cf/openai/whisper-large-v3-turbo", {audio: base64}, {gateway})`, neurons added to `llm_usage` (reported value, else an estimate from duration); AI error → 502 `transcribe_failed`. Returns `{text}`.

### 2. Web
- `web/hooks/useRecorder.ts`: picks `audio/webm;codecs=opus` → `audio/ogg;codecs=opus` → `audio/mp4` (Safari), 24 kbps, auto-stop at 30 s, stops the mic tracks, exposes elapsed seconds and state (idle / recording / transcribing / error, including permission denied).
- `web/lib/api.ts` `transcribe(blob)` with error text for each error code.
- `web/components/VoiceButton.tsx`: mic button (`aria-pressed`, label "Start voice input" / "Stop recording"), "0:12 / 0:30" while recording, `DashedLoader` while transcribing; hidden when `MediaRecorder` isn't supported.
- `Composer.tsx`: optional voice slot beside Send; the transcript fills the box (appended after a space if there's text), focuses it, never submits. Used by `NewIncident.tsx` and `IncidentView.tsx`.

### 3. Tests
- Unit `voice-normalize.test.ts` (the done-when phrase → "is demo.example.com down", slashes, casing, punctuation); web unit for MIME pick and `transcribe` error mapping.
- Integration `voice.test.ts`: 415, 413 (header and real size), 400 empty, mock text returned, 6th in a minute → 429, D1 hourly window counted under kind `voice`, budget guard → 503. Add `VOICE_LIMIT` to the integration miniflare rate limits if it isn't already there.
- **Done when:** local headless Chrome with fake mic flags (`--use-fake-device-for-media-stream --use-fake-ui-for-media-stream`, `playwright-core` from the scratchpad as before), mock mode: click mic → stop → the input reads "is demo.example.com down" and no run starts. Optional live check: macOS `say` → `.m4a` → `POST /api/voice` under `LLM_MODE=live` (a few neurons), only with the user's OK.

- Live checks after the user's next `dev` → `main` merge deploys: cert/provider cards on a real broken host, Cache API on workers.dev, postmortem step 6 on the live walkthrough.

```
async function readCapped(res: Response, cap: number): Promise<{ body: string; truncated: boolean }> {
  if (!res.body) return { body: "", truncated: false };
  const reader = res.body.getReader();
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let size = 0;
  let truncated = false;
  for (;;) {
    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    if (done) break;
    const room = cap - size;
    if (value.byteLength > room) {
      chunks.push(value.subarray(0, room));
      size += room;
      truncated = true;
      break;
    }
    chunks.push(value);
    size += value.byteLength;
  }
  if (truncated) await reader.cancel().catch(() => undefined);
  const all = new Uint8Array(size);
  let offset = 0;
  for (const c of chunks) {
    all.set(c, offset);
    offset += c.byteLength;
  }
  return { body: new TextDecoder().decode(all), truncated };
}

export type Hop = { url: string; status: number; location: string | null; ttfbMs: number; totalMs: number };
export type HopStop = "done" | "loop" | "max_hops" | "blocked" | "error";
export type HopResult = {
  hops: Hop[];
  /** Last response read (the final page, or the last redirect when stopped early). */
  final: ReadOk | null;
  stop: HopStop;
  blocked?: GuardFail & { url: string };
  error?: ReadErr | { ok: false; url: string; error: "bad_location"; message: string };
};

const REDIRECTS = new Set([301, 302, 303, 307, 308]);

/** Follows redirects by hand: at most 1 + MAX_HOPS requests, guard-checked each time, stopping on a loop. */
export async function followHops(fetchFn: Fetch, guard: Guard, startUrl: string, method: Method = "GET", opts: ReadOptions = {}): Promise<HopResult> {
  const hops: Hop[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  let current = startUrl;
  let final: ReadOk | null = null;

  for (let i = 0; i <= MAX_HOPS; i++) {
    if (seen.has(current)) return { hops, final, stop: "loop" };
    seen.add(current);

    const checked = await guard(current);
    if (!checked.ok) return { hops, final, stop: "blocked", blocked: { ...checked, url: current } };

    const res = await readOnce(fetchFn, checked.url.toString(), method, opts);
    if (!res.ok) return { hops, final, stop: "error", error: res };
    final = res;
    hops.push({ url: res.url, status: res.status, location: res.location, ttfbMs: res.ttfbMs, totalMs: res.totalMs });

    if (!REDIRECTS.has(res.status) || !res.location) return { hops, final, stop: "done" };
    try {
      current = new URL(res.location, res.url).toString();
    } catch {
      return { hops, final, stop: "error", error: { ok: false, url: res.url, error: "bad_location", message: res.location } };
    }
  }
  return { hops, final, stop: "max_hops" };
}
import type { Fetch } from "../../../worker/lib/doh";

export type Handler = (url: URL, init?: RequestInit) => Response | Promise<Response>;

/** A fetch stub that records every call and dispatches to `handler`. */
export function fakeFetch(handler: Handler): Fetch & { calls: string[] } {
  const calls: string[] = [];
  const fn = (async (input: string, init?: RequestInit) => {
    calls.push(input);
    return handler(new URL(input), init);
  }) as Fetch & { calls: string[] };
  fn.calls = calls;
  return fn;
}

/** Dispatches by hostname; unknown hosts fail like a dead network. */
export function byHost(hosts: Record<string, Handler>): Handler {
  return (url, init) => {
    const h = hosts[url.hostname];
    if (!h) return Promise.reject(new TypeError(`no route to ${url.hostname}`));
    return h(url, init);
  };
}

export type Records = { status?: number; A?: string[]; AAAA?: string[]; CNAME?: string[]; MX?: string[] };
const TYPE = { A: 1, AAAA: 28, CNAME: 5, MX: 15 } as const;

/** Answers DoH JSON queries from a host → records table; unknown hosts get NXDOMAIN. */
export function dohHandler(zone: Record<string, Records>): Handler {
  return (url) => {
    const name = url.searchParams.get("name") ?? "";
    const type = (url.searchParams.get("type") ?? "A") as keyof typeof TYPE;
    const rec = zone[name];
    if (!rec) return Response.json({ Status: 3 });
    if (rec.status && rec.status !== 0) return Response.json({ Status: rec.status });
    const answers = [
      ...(type === "CNAME" ? [] : (rec.CNAME ?? [])).map((data) => ({ name, type: TYPE.CNAME, TTL: 300, data })),
      ...((rec[type] as string[] | undefined) ?? []).map((data) => ({ name, type: TYPE[type], TTL: 300, data })),
    ];
    return Response.json({ Status: 0, Answer: answers });
  };
}

/** Manually advanced clock for latency assertions. */
export function fakeClock(start = 1_000_000) {
  let t = start;
  return { now: () => t, advance: (ms: number) => void (t += ms) };
}

import { CATEGORIES, type CertRaw, type Provider, type ProviderRaw } from "../../worker/diagnosis/categories";

// Builders: healthy by default, each option breaks one thing.

type DnsOpts = { id?: string; rcode?: [string, string]; addresses?: [string[], string[]]; cname?: string[]; mismatch?: boolean; addressesDiffer?: boolean };

function dns(o: DnsOpts = {}): ToolResult<DnsRaw> {
  const [cfCode, gCode] = o.rcode ?? ["NOERROR", "NOERROR"];
  const [cfAddrs, gAddrs] = o.addresses ?? [["93.184.215.14"], ["93.184.215.14"]];
  const typeResult = (code: string, addrs: string[]): TypeResult =>
    code === "ERROR" ? { status: "ERROR", error: "resolver down", answers: [] } : { status: code, answers: addrs.map((data) => ({ type: "A", data, ttl: 300 })) };
  const answered = [cfCode, gCode].some((c) => c !== "ERROR");
  const status: CheckStatus = !answered ? "unavailable" : o.mismatch || cfCode !== "NOERROR" ? "fail" : "pass";
  return {
    evidenceId: o.id ?? "dns-1",
    tool: "dns_lookup",
    ok: status !== "unavailable",
    status,
    summary: "dns summary",
    digest: "{}",
    raw: {
      hostname: "shop.example.com",
      types: ["A"],
      resolvers: { cloudflare: { A: typeResult(cfCode, cfAddrs) }, google: { A: typeResult(gCode, gAddrs) } },
      addresses: { cloudflare: cfCode === "NOERROR" ? cfAddrs : [], google: gCode === "NOERROR" ? gAddrs : [] },
      cnameChain: o.cname ?? [],
      cnameTargets: o.cname ?? [],
      mismatch: o.mismatch ?? false,
      addressesDiffer: o.addressesDiffer ?? false,
      ssrfBlocked: false,
    },
  };
}

type HttpOpts = {
  id?: string;
  /** One entry per sample; null = the request failed (see `error`). */
  statuses?: (number | null)[];
  error?: "timeout" | "network";
  message?: string;
  stop?: "loop" | "max_hops";
  totalMs?: number;
  ttfbMs?: number;
  server?: string;
  cfRay?: string;
  asset?: HttpRaw["asset"];
  refused?: HttpRaw["refused"];
  summary?: string;
};

function http(o: HttpOpts = {}): ToolResult<HttpRaw> {
  const url = "https://shop.example.com/";
  const total = o.totalMs ?? 120;
  const ttfb = o.ttfbMs ?? 80;
  let samples: Sample[];

function cert(days: number, sanMatch = true): ToolResult<CertRaw> {
  return {
    evidenceId: "cert-1",
    tool: "cert_info",
    ok: true,
    status: days < 0 ? "fail" : "pass",
    summary: "cert summary",
    digest: "{}",
    raw: { hostname: "shop.example.com", certs: [{ issuer: "R3", notBefore: "2026-07-01", notAfter: "2026-10-01", daysToExpiry: days, sanMatch }] },
  };
}

function provider(active: Partial<Record<Provider, string[]>>): ToolResult<ProviderRaw> {
  const providers: ProviderRaw["providers"] = {};
  for (const [p, names] of Object.entries(active)) providers[p as Provider] = { active: names.map((name) => ({ name })) };
  return { evidenceId: "status-1", tool: "provider_status", ok: true, status: "pass", summary: "status summary", digest: "{}", raw: { providers } };
}

const goodAsset = { url: "https://shop.example.com/static/app.js", status: 200, cacheControl: "public, max-age=31536000, immutable", setCookie: false };

describe("classify: one fixture per category", () => {
  const cases: [string, ToolResult[], { category: string; confidence: string; evidence: string[] }][] = [
    ["dns_failure (NXDOMAIN)", [dns({ rcode: ["NXDOMAIN", "NXDOMAIN"], addresses: [[], []] }), http({ statuses: [null, null, null] })], { category: "dns_failure", confidence: "high", evidence: ["dns-1"] }],
    ["tls_expiry (expired cert)", [dns(), http(), cert(-3)], { category: "tls_expiry", confidence: "high", evidence: ["cert-1"] }],
    ["redirect_loop", [dns(), http({ stop: "loop" })], { category: "redirect_loop", confidence: "high", evidence: ["http-1"] }],
    ["edge_error", [dns(), http({ statuses: [522, 522, 522], server: "cloudflare" })], { category: "edge_error", confidence: "high", evidence: ["http-1"] }],
    ["provider_outage", [dns(), http({ statuses: [500, 500, 500], server: "cloudflare" }), provider({ cloudflare: ["Elevated 5xx errors in LHR"] })], { category: "provider_outage", confidence: "medium", evidence: ["http-1", "status-1"] }],
    ["origin_5xx (demo errors)", [dns(), http({ statuses: [500, 500, 500] })], { category: "origin_5xx", confidence: "high", evidence: ["http-1"] }],
    ["high_latency (demo slow)", [dns(), http({ totalMs: 3100, ttfbMs: 3050, asset: goodAsset })], { category: "high_latency", confidence: "high", evidence: ["http-1"] }],
    ["cache_misconfiguration (demo bad_cache)", [dns(), http({ asset: { ...goodAsset, cacheControl: "no-store", setCookie: true } })], { category: "cache_misconfiguration", confidence: "high", evidence: ["http-1"] }],
    ["dns_mismatch", [dns({ rcode: ["NOERROR", "NXDOMAIN"], addresses: [["93.184.215.14"], []], mismatch: true }), http({ statuses: [404, 404, 404] })], { category: "dns_mismatch", confidence: "medium", evidence: ["dns-1"] }],
    ["healthy (demo healthy)", [dns(), http({ asset: goodAsset })], { category: "healthy", confidence: "high", evidence: ["http-1", "dns-1"] }],
    ["inconclusive (timeouts with good DNS)", [dns(), http({ statuses: [null, null, null], error: "timeout" })], { category: "inconclusive", confidence: "low", evidence: ["http-1", "dns-1"] }],
  ];

  it("covers every category", () => {
    expect(new Set(cases.map(([, , want]) => want.category))).toEqual(new Set(CATEGORIES));
  });

  it.each(cases)("%s", (_name, evidence, want) => {
    const f = classify(evidence);
    expect(f).toMatchObject(want);
    for (const id of want.evidence) expect(f.cause).toContain(`[${id}]`);
    expect(f.cause).toMatch(/\]\.$/);
    expect(f.next_steps.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
  });
});

describe("classify: precedence (first match wins)", () => {
  const cat = (evidence: ToolResult[]) => classify(evidence).category;

  it("dns_failure beats an HTTP failure", () => {
    expect(cat([dns({ rcode: ["SERVFAIL", "SERVFAIL"], addresses: [[], []] }), http({ statuses: [500, 500, 500] })])).toBe("dns_failure");
  });
  it("a TLS handshake error is tls_expiry, ahead of everything after it", () => {
    const f = classify([dns(), http({ statuses: [null, null, null], error: "network", message: "TLS peer's certificate is not trusted" })]);
    expect(f).toMatchObject({ category: "tls_expiry", confidence: "high", evidence: ["http-1"] });
  });
  it("redirect_loop beats 5xx", () => {
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ stop: "max_hops" })])).toBe("redirect_loop");
  });
  it("52x from Cloudflare is edge_error; the same codes without server: cloudflare are origin_5xx", () => {
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ statuses: [521, 521, 521], server: "cloudflare" })])).toBe("edge_error");
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ statuses: [521, 521, 521], server: "nginx" })])).toBe("origin_5xx");
  });
  it("edge_error needs two edge samples (one bad sample isn't healthy either)", () => {
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ statuses: [522, 200, 200], server: "cloudflare" })])).toBe("inconclusive");
  });
  it("provider incident + 5xx beats origin_5xx; no incident falls through", () => {
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ statuses: [503, 503, 503], cfRay: "abc-LHR" }), provider({ cloudflare: ["Outage"] })])).toBe("provider_outage");
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ statuses: [503, 503, 503], cfRay: "abc-LHR" }), provider({ cloudflare: [] })])).toBe("origin_5xx");
  });
  it("provider incident only counts for the fingerprinted provider", () => {
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ statuses: [500, 500, 500], server: "nginx" }), provider({ cloudflare: ["Outage"] })])).toBe("origin_5xx");
    const gh = classify([dns({ cname: ["user.github.io"] }), http({ statuses: [null, null, null] }), provider({ github: ["Pages degraded"] })]);
    expect(gh).toMatchObject({ category: "provider_outage", evidence: ["http-1", "status-1", "dns-1"] });
    expect(cat([dns({ cname: ["x.s3.amazonaws.com"] }), http({ statuses: [500, 500, 500] }), provider({ aws: ["S3 errors"] })])).toBe("provider_outage");
  });
  it("provider incident with healthy HTTP is still healthy", () => {
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ server: "cloudflare" }), provider({ cloudflare: ["Outage"] })])).toBe("healthy");
  });
  it("slow + bad cache is high_latency", () => {
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ totalMs: 2500, asset: { ...goodAsset, cacheControl: "private" } })])).toBe("high_latency");
  });
  it("TTFB alone can make it high_latency", () => {
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ totalMs: 1600, ttfbMs: 1500 })])).toBe("high_latency");
    expect(cat([dns(), http({ totalMs: 1999, ttfbMs: 1499 })])).toBe("healthy");
  });
  it("mismatch + 5xx is origin_5xx (mismatch is a late rule)", () => {
    expect(cat([dns({ rcode: ["NOERROR", "SERVFAIL"], addresses: [["1.2.3.4"], []], mismatch: true }), http({ statuses: [500, 500, 500] })])).toBe("origin_5xx");
  });
});

describe("classify: edge cases", () => {
  it("different address sets alone (geo/CDN DNS) are healthy, not dns_mismatch", () => {
    expect(classify([dns({ addresses: [["1.1.1.1"], ["2.2.2.2"]], addressesDiffer: true }), http()]).category).toBe("healthy");
  });
  it("a resolver outage isn't a DNS failure", () => {
    expect(classify([dns({ rcode: ["ERROR", "ERROR"], addresses: [[], []] }), http({ statuses: [null, null, null] })]).category).toBe("inconclusive");
  });
  it("NXDOMAIN from one resolver while the other is down isn't dns_failure", () => {
    expect(classify([dns({ rcode: ["NXDOMAIN", "ERROR"], addresses: [[], []] })]).category).not.toBe("dns_failure");
  });
  it("NOERROR with no addresses from both resolvers is dns_failure", () => {
    const f = classify([dns({ addresses: [[], []] })]);
    expect(f).toMatchObject({ category: "dns_failure", confidence: "high" });
    expect(f.cause).toContain("Neither DNS resolver returns an A or AAAA record");
  });
  it("without a DNS check, a probe refused for no_addresses is dns_failure", () => {
    const f = classify([http({ refused: { code: "no_addresses", message: "shop.example.com has no A/AAAA records" } })]);
    expect(f).toMatchObject({ category: "dns_failure", evidence: ["http-1"] });
  });
  it("a probe refused for a private address is inconclusive", () => {
    expect(classify([dns(), http({ refused: { code: "private_address", message: "resolves to 10.0.0.9" } })]).category).toBe("inconclusive");
  });
  it("an opaque connection error isn't a TLS failure", () => {
    expect(classify([dns(), http({ statuses: [null, null, null], error: "network", message: "internal error; reference = abc" })]).category).toBe("inconclusive");
  });
  it("a cert expiring within 7 days is tls_expiry medium only when HTTP is healthy", () => {
    expect(classify([dns(), http(), cert(5)])).toMatchObject({ category: "tls_expiry", confidence: "medium", evidence: ["cert-1", "http-1"] });
    expect(classify([dns(), http({ statuses: [500, 500, 500] }), cert(5)]).category).toBe("origin_5xx");
    expect(classify([dns(), http(), cert(30)]).category).toBe("healthy");
  });
  it("only certificates that match the hostname count", () => {
    expect(classify([dns(), http(), cert(-3, false)]).category).toBe("healthy");
  });
  it("2 of 3 samples 5xx is origin_5xx with medium confidence; 1 of 3 is inconclusive", () => {
    expect(classify([dns(), http({ statuses: [500, 502, 200] })])).toMatchObject({ category: "origin_5xx", confidence: "medium" });
    expect(classify([dns(), http({ statuses: [500, 200, 200] })]).category).toBe("inconclusive");
  });
  it("4xx is inconclusive", () => {
    expect(classify([dns(), http({ statuses: [404, 404, 404], summary: "HTTP 404/404/404." })])).toMatchObject({ category: "inconclusive", confidence: "low" });
  });
  it("no evidence at all is inconclusive and cites nothing", () => {
    const f = classify([]);
    expect(f).toMatchObject({ category: "inconclusive", confidence: "low", evidence: [] });
    expect(f.cause).not.toContain("[");
  });
  it("missing cert and provider evidence never throws", () => {
    expect(() => classify([http({ statuses: [500, 500, 500], server: "cloudflare" })])).not.toThrow();
  });
  it("uses the latest HTTP result", () => {
    const f = classify([dns(), http({ id: "http-1", statuses: [500, 500, 500] }), http({ id: "http-2" })]);
    expect(f).toMatchObject({ category: "healthy", evidence: ["http-2", "dns-1"] });
  });
  it("an asset probe that errored doesn't count as bad caching", () => {
    expect(classify([dns(), http({ asset: { url: goodAsset.url, error: "budget" } })]).category).toBe("healthy");
  });
  it("proposes restore_demo_service for everything except healthy", () => {
    expect(classify([dns(), http()]).actions).toEqual([]);
    expect(classify([dns(), http({ statuses: [500, 500, 500] })]).actions).toEqual(["restore_demo_service"]);
    expect(classify([]).actions).toEqual(["restore_demo_service"]);
  });
});
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { evidenceId, llmTools, runTool } from "../../worker/tools/registry";
import { byHost, dohHandler, fakeFetch } from "./helpers/fake-fetch";

const scope = { host: "shop.example.com", cnameTargets: ["shop.example.com.cdn.cloudflare.net"] };
const zone = { "shop.example.com": { A: ["93.184.215.14"] } };
const ctx = () => ({
  fetch: fakeFetch(byHost({ "cloudflare-dns.com": dohHandler(zone), "dns.google": dohHandler(zone), "shop.example.com": () => new Response("ok") })),
  evidenceId: "x-1",
});

describe("registry", () => {
  it("builds evidence IDs", () => {
    expect(evidenceId("dns_lookup", 1)).toBe("dns-1");
    expect(evidenceId("http_probe", 12)).toBe("http-12");
    expect(evidenceId("provider_status", 2)).toBe("status-2");
  });

  it("exposes OpenAI-style tool definitions with optional defaults", () => {
    const tools = llmTools();
    expect(tools.map((t) => t.function.name)).toEqual(["dns_lookup", "http_probe", "submit_findings"]);
    const dns = tools[0]!.function.parameters as { required: string[]; properties: Record<string, unknown> };
    expect(dns.required).toEqual(["hostname"]);
    expect(dns.properties).toHaveProperty("types");
    expect(tools[0]!.function.parameters).not.toHaveProperty("$schema");
  });

  it("offers only submit_findings on the last turn", () => {
    expect(llmTools({ finishOnly: true }).map((t) => t.function.name)).toEqual(["submit_findings"]);
  });

  it("sends no regex patterns (glm rejects look-around in its tool grammar)", () => {
    expect(JSON.stringify(llmTools())).not.toContain('"pattern"');
    expect(JSON.stringify(llmTools()[0]!.function.parameters)).toContain("A public hostname");
  });

  it("runs a valid, in-scope call and counts subrequests", async () => {
    const out = await runTool("dns_lookup", { hostname: "shop.example.com", types: ["A"] }, ctx(), scope);
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ ok: true, subrequests: 2, cap: 2, result: { status: "pass" } });
  });

  it.each([
    ["dns_lookup", { hostname: 42 }, /Invalid arguments/],
    ["dns_lookup", { hostname: "10.0.0.1" }, /Invalid arguments/],
    ["http_probe", { url: "ftp://shop.example.com/" }, /Invalid arguments/],
    ["http_probe", { url: "https://other.example.com/" }, /limited to shop\.example\.com/],
    ["dns_lookup", { hostname: "attacker.example.net" }, /isn't one/],
    ["rm_rf", {}, /Unknown tool/],
  ])("reports %s %j as invalid", async (name, args, message) => {
    const out = await runTool(name, args, ctx(), scope);
    expect(out.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(!out.ok && out.invalid).toMatch(message);
  });

  it("enforces the per-tool cap", async () => {
    const out = await runTool("http_probe", { url: "https://shop.example.com/" }, ctx(), scope);
    expect(out.ok && out.subrequests).toBeLessThanOrEqual(out.ok ? out.cap : 0);
  });
});

[hostname, `*.${labels.slice(1).join(\".\")}`] : [hostname];\n}\n\n/** A SAN matches the host itself, or as a wildcard one label up (`*.example.com` covers `shop.example.com`). */\nexport function sanMatches(hostname: string, name: string): boolean {\n  const n = name.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\\.$/, \"\");\n  if (n === hostname) return true;\n  if (!n.startsWith(\"*.\")) return false;\n  const dot = hostname.indexOf(\".\");\n  return dot > 0 && hostname.slice(dot + 1) === n.slice(2);\n}\n\n/**\n * crt.sh JSON, at most `CRT_MAX_ENTRIES` entries. Null when it isn't a JSON array.\n */\nexport function parseCrt(body: string, truncated: boolean): CrtEntry[] | null {\n  const asArray = (text: string) => {\n    const j: unknown = JSON.parse(text);\n    return Array.isArray(j) ? (j.slice(0, CRT_MAX_ENTRIES) as CrtEntry[]) : null;\n  };\n  try {\n    return asArray(body);\n  } catch {\n    if (!truncated) return null;\n  }\n  const cut = body.lastIndexOf(\"},{\");\n  if (cut < 0) return null;\n  try {\n    return asArray(`${body.slice(0, cut + 1)}]`);\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/** The issuer's CN (\"Let's Encrypt YE2\" style) rather than the whole DN. */\nfunction issuerName(dn: string): string {\n  const cn = /(?:^|,\\s*)CN=([^,]+)/.exec(dn)?.[1];\n  const org = /(?:^|,\\s*)O=([^,]+)/.exec(dn)?.[1];\n  if (cn && org && !cn.includes(org)) return `${org} ${cn}`.slice(0, 120);\n  return (cn ?? dn).slice(0, 120);\n}\n\n/** crt.sh times are UTC without a zone. */\nconst utc = (t: string) => Date.parse(/[zZ]|[+-]\\d\\d:?\\d\\d$/.test(t) ? t : `${t}Z`);\n\nfunction toCert(e: CrtEntry, hostname: string, now: number): Cert | null {\n  if (!e.not_after || !e.not_before) return null;\n  const notAfter = utc(e.not_after);\n  if (Number.isNaN(notAfter)) return null;\n  const names = [...(e.name_value ?? \"\").split(\"\\n\"), e.common_name ?? \"\"].filter(Boolean);\n  return {\n    issuer: issuerName(e.issuer_name ?? \"unknown\"),\n    notBefore: e.not_before,\n    notAfter: e.not_after,\n    daysToExpiry: Math.floor((notAfter - now) / DAY_MS),\n    sanMatch: names.some((n) => sanMatches(hostname, n)),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction defaultCache(): Pick<Cache, \"match\" | \"put\"> | null {\n  try {\n    return (globalThis as { caches?: CacheStorage & { default?: Cache } }).caches?.default ?? null;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\ntype Fetched = { ok: true; entries: CrtEntry[] } | { ok: false; reason: string };\n\n/** One crt.sh query, through an hour's cache. Every cache call is best effort: a broken cache never fails the check. */\nasync function queryCrt(ctx: ToolContext, url: string, now: () => number): Promise<Fetched> {\n  const cache = ctx.cache === undefined ? defaultCache() : ctx.cache;\n  try {\n    const hit = await cache?.match(url);\n    if (hit) {\n      const entries = parseCrt(await hit.text(), true);\n      if (entries) return { ok: true, entries };\n    }\n  } catch {\n    // ignore: fetch instead\n  }\n  const res = await readOnce(ctx.fetch, url, \"GET\", { readCap: CRT_READ_CAP, timeoutMs: CRT_TIMEOUT_MS, now });\n  if (!res.ok) return { ok: false, reason: res.error };\n  if (res.status !== 200) return { ok: false, reason: `HTTP ${res.status}` };\n  const entries = parseCrt(res.body, res.truncated);\n  if (!entries) return { ok: false, reason: \"not JSON\" };\n  try {\n    await cache?.put(url, new Response(JSON.stringify(entries), { headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\", \"cache-control\": `max-age=${CRT_CACHE_TTL_S}` } }));\n  } catch {\n    // ignore: the next run asks crt.sh again\n  }\n  return { ok: true, entries };\n}\n\n/**\n * `cert_info` (TDD §7.3): unexpired certificates for the host from certificate-transparency logs (crt.sh).\n * Best effort: CT logs show certificates that were issued, not necessarily the one being served, and crt.sh is\n * often slow or down, which makes the check `unavailable`, never a failure (PRD R4).\n */\nexport async function runCertInfo(input: CertInput, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult<CertRaw>> {\n  const now = ctx.now ?? (() => Date.now());\n  const queries = certQueries(input.hostname);\n  const answers = await Promise.all(queries.map(async (q) => ({ q, r: await queryCrt(ctx, crtUrl(q), now) })));\n  const failed = answers.filter((a) => !a.r.ok).map((a) => a.q);\n\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n  const certs: Cert[] = [];\n  for (const { r } of answers) {\n    if (!r.ok) continue;\n    for (const e of r.entries) {\n      const key = e.serial_number ?? String(e.id ?? \"\");\n      if (key && seen.has(key)) continue;\n      seen.add(key);\n      const c = toCert(e, input.hostname, now());\n      if (c) certs.push(c);\n    }\n  }\n  // Matching certificates first, then the most days left.\n  certs.sort((a, b) => Number(b.sanMatch) - Number(a.sanMatch) || b.daysToExpiry - a.daysToExpiry);\n  const raw: CertRaw = { hostname: input.hostname, certs: certs.slice(0, 3), queries, ...(failed.length ? { failed } : {}) };\n\n  const { status, summary } = judge(raw, failed.length === queries.length);\n  return {\n    evidenceId: ctx.evidenceId,\n    tool: \"cert_info\",\n    ok: status === \"pass\" || status === \"fail\",\n    status,\n    summary,\n    digest: fitDigest({ host: raw.hostname, source: \"CT logs (crt.sh), best effort\", certs: raw.certs, failed: raw.failed }, [\"failed\"]),\n    raw,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction judge(raw: CertRaw, allFailed: boolean): { status: CheckStatus; summary: string } {\n  const prefix = \"CT logs (best effort):\";\n  if (allFailed) return { status: \"unavailable\", summary: \"Certificate info unavailable: crt.sh didn't answer.\" };\n  const best = raw.certs.find((c) => c.sanMatch);\n  if (!best) {\n    // A missing answer might have held the certificate: don't call it missing.\n    if (raw.failed?.length) return { status: \"unavailable\", summary: `Certificate info incomplete: crt.sh didn't answer for ${raw.failed.join(\", \")}.` };\n    return { status: \"fail\", summary: `${prefix} no unexpired certificate covers ${raw.hostname}.` };\n  }\n  const until = best.notAfter.slice(0, 10);\n  if (best.daysToExpiry < 0) return { status: \"fail\", summary: `${prefix} the newest certificate expired on ${until}.` };\n  if (best.daysToExpiry <= CERT_WARN_DAYS) return { status: \"fail\", summary: `${prefix} the newest certificate expires in ${best.daysToExpiry} day(s), on ${until} (${best.issuer}).` };\n  return { status: \"pass\", summary: `${prefix} valid until ${until} (${best.daysToExpiry} days), issued by ${best.issuer}.` };\n}\nEOF\nnpx tsc -p tsconfig.worker.json --noEmit 2>&1 | head",

AWS's feed was 231 KB of UTF-16 in the WP-21 spike, so it's read in part. */\nconst STATUSPAGE_CAP = 64 * 1024;\nconst AWS_CAP = 128 * 1024;\nconst STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS = 5_000;\nconst MAX_PER_PROVIDER = 3;\n\nexport const providerInput = z.object({\n  providers: z\n    .array(z.enum(PROVIDERS))\n    .min(1)\n    .max(PROVIDERS.length)\n    .transform((ps) => [...new Set(ps)]),\n});\nexport type ProviderInput = z.output<typeof providerInput>;\n\n/** UTF-16 with a byte-order mark (AWS sends big-endian); UTF-8 without one. */\nexport function decodeText(bytes: Uint8Array): string {\n  const be = bytes[0] === 0xfe && bytes[1] === 0xff;\n  const le = bytes[0] === 0xff && bytes[1] === 0xfe;\n  if (!be && !le) return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);\n  const n = Math.floor((bytes.length - 2) / 2);\n  const units = new Uint16Array(n);\n  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n    const a = bytes[2 + 2 * i]!;\n    const b = bytes[3 + 2 * i]!;\n    units[i] = be ? (a << 8) | b : (b << 8) | a;\n  }\n  let out = \"\";\n  for (let i = 0; i < n; i += 4096) out += String.fromCharCode(...units.subarray(i, i + 4096));\n  return out;\n}\n\nconst clip = (s: string, n = 120) => s.replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim().slice(0, n);\n\n/** Statuspage `incidents/unresolved.json`. */\nexport function parseStatuspage(body: string): ProviderIncident[] | null {\n  try {\n    const j = JSON.parse(body) as { incidents?: { name?: string; status?: string; impact?: string; shortlink?: string }[] };\n    if (!Array.isArray(j.incidents)) return null;\n    return j.incidents.map((i) => ({\n      name: clip(i.name ?? \"Unnamed incident\"),\n      ...(i.status ? { status: i.status } : {}),\n      ...(i.impact ? { impact: i.impact } : {}),\n      ...(i.shortlink ? { url: i.shortlink } : {}),\n    }));\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\ntype AwsEvent = { service_name?: string; summary?: string; region_name?: string; status?: string };\nconst AWS_EVENT = /\"region_name\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]*)\"\\s*,\\s*\"status\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]*)\"\\s*,\\s*\"service\"\\s*:\\s*\"[^\"]*\"\\s*,\\s*\"service_name\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]*)\"\\s*,\\s*\"summary\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]*)\"/g;\n\n/**\n * AWS's current events, best effort. The whole feed parses when it fits the read cap; otherwise the events are\n * picked out of the part that was read (each event's first fields come before its long `event_log`).\n */\nexport function parseAws(body: string, truncated: boolean): ProviderIncident[] | null {\n  const toIncident = (e: AwsEvent): ProviderIncident => ({\n    name: clip(`${e.service_name ?? \"AWS\"}: ${e.summary ?? \"event\"}${e.region_name ? ` (${e.region_name})` : \"\"}`),\n    ...(e.status ? { status: e.status } : {}),\n  });\n  const text = body.trim();\n  if (!truncated) {\n    try {\n      const j: unknown = JSON.parse(text);\n      return Array.isArray(j) ? (j as AwsEvent[]).map(toIncident) : null;\n    } catch {\n      return null;\n    }\n  }\n  if (!text.startsWith(\"[\")) return null;\n  return [...text.matchAll(AWS_EVENT)].map((m) => toIncident({ region_name: m[1], status: m[2], service_name: m[3], summary: m[4] }));\n}\n\ntype Answer = { provider: Provider; active: ProviderIncident[] | null };\n\nasync function check(ctx: ToolContext, provider: Provider, now: () => number): Promise<Answer> {\n  const aws = provider === \"aws\";\n  const res = await readOnce(ctx.fetch, STATUS_URLS[provider], \"GET\", {\n    readCap: aws ? AWS_CAP : STATUSPAGE_CAP,\n    timeoutMs: STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS,\n    now,\n    ...(aws ? { decode: decodeText } : {}),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok || res.status !== 200) return { provider, active: null };\n  return { provider, active: aws ? parseAws(res.body, res.truncated) : parseStatuspage(res.body) };\n}\n\n/**\n * `provider_status` (TDD §7.3): \"is it me or them?\". Active incidents on each provider's public status page.\n * Only major/critical Statuspage incidents count as an outage (`isSignificant`); minor ones are listed.\n */\nexport async function runProviderStatus(input: ProviderInput, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolResult<ProviderRaw>> {\n  const now = ctx.now ?? (() => Date.now());\n  const answers = await Promise.all(\n    input.providers.map((p) =>\n      check(ctx, p, now).catch((e: Error) => {\n        if (e.name === \"BudgetExceededError\") throw e;\n        return { provider: p, active: null };\n      }),\n    ),\n  );\n  const raw: ProviderRaw = { providers: {} };\n  const unavailable: Provider[] = [];\n  for (const a of answers) {\n    if (a.active === null) unavailable.push(a.provider);\n    else raw.providers[a.provider] = { active: a.active.slice(0, MAX_PER_PROVIDER) };\n  }\n  if (unavailable.length) raw.unavailable = unavailable;\n\n  const { status, summary } = judge(raw, answers.map((a) => a.provider));\n  return {\n    evidenceId: ctx.evidenceId,\n    tool: \"provider_status\",\n    ok: status === \"pass\" || status === \"fail\",\n    status,\n    summary,\n    digest: fitDigest({ providers: raw.providers, unavailable: raw.unavailable, counts: \"only major/critical incidents count as an outage\" }, [\"counts\"]),\n    raw,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction judge(raw: ProviderRaw, asked: Provider[]): { status: CheckStatus; summary: string } {\n  const checked = asked.filter((p) => raw.providers[p]);\n  if (checked.length === 0) return { status: \"unavailable\", summary: `Status pages didn't answer (${asked.map((p) => NAMES[p]).join(\", \")}).` };\n  const parts = checked.map((p) => {\n    const active = raw.providers[p]!.active;\n    const major = active.filter(isSignificant);\n    if (major.length) return `${NAMES[p]}: ${major.length} active incident(s), \"${major[0]!.name}\"`;\n    if (active.length) return `${NAMES[p]}: no outage (${active.length} minor incident(s))`;\n    return `${NAMES[p]}: no active incidents`;\n  });\n  if (raw.unavailable?.length) parts.push(`${raw.unavailable.map((p) => NAMES[p]).join(\", \")}: no answer`);\n  const outage = checked.some((p) => raw.providers[p]!.active.some(isSignificant));\n  return { status: outage ? \"fail\" : \"pass\", summary: `${parts.join(\" · \")}.` };\n}\nEOF\nnpx tsc -p tsconfig.worker.json --noEmit 2>&1 | head",
  "description": "Write provider_status tool"
}

    const runFixed = async (calls: ValidCall[], turn: number, stepName: string) => {
      if (!calls.length || lastAt >= deadline || budget.remaining() < 1 + Math.min(...calls.map((c) => c.cap))) return;
      const ids = await io.step(stepName, () => io.agent.addPendingChecks(runId, turn, calls.map(({ tool, args }) => ({ tool, args }))));

      for (const [i, call] of calls.entries()) {
        if (lastAt >= deadline) break;
        await runCheck(ids[i]!, call);
      }
    };

    // WP-13b: live models planned one check per turn, so every run spent a turn per check and hit the
    // turn cap. A new incident always needs DNS + HTTP, so they run first (the first card shows in about

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { RunContext } from "../../worker/agent/IncidentAgent";
import type { Findings } from "../../worker/diagnosis/categories";
import { LlmUnavailable, type LlmRequest, type LlmResult } from "../../worker/llm/models";
import { salvageFindings } from "../../worker/loop/findings";
import { DEFAULT_LIMITS, diagnose, validateCalls, type ApprovalDecision, type DiagnoseLimits, type RunIO } from "../../worker/loop/plan";
import type { ToolName, ToolResult } from "../../worker/tools/types";

const TARGET = "https://shop.example.com/";
const HOST = "shop.example.com";

type Reply = LlmResult | Error;
const reply = (...calls: [string, unknown][]): LlmResult => ({
  toolCalls: calls.map(([name, args], i) => ({ id: `c${i}`, name, args })),
  usage: { in: 0, out: 0, neurons: 1 },
  model: "gpt",
  fallback: false,
});
const dns = (): [string, unknown] => ["dns_lookup", { hostname: HOST }];
const http = (path = ""): [string, unknown] => ["http_probe", { url: `${TARGET}${path}` }];
const submit = (evidence: string[], extra: Partial<Findings> = {}): [string, unknown] => [
  "submit_findings",
  { category: "origin_5xx", confidence: "high", evidence, cause: "The origin fails.", next_steps: ["Check logs."], actions: [], ...extra },
];

const RULES: Findings = { category: "inconclusive", confidence: "low", evidence: [], cause: "Rules.", next_steps: ["Retry."], actions: ["restore_demo_service"] };

/** A fake Workflow: steps run inline, an in-memory agent, scripted model turns and a clock that ticks per step. */
function harness(
  o: {
    replies?: Reply[];
    tool?: (name: ToolName, id: string) => ToolResult | Error;
    ctx?: Partial<RunContext>;
    tick?: number;
    failSetFindings?: boolean;
    /** A card is proposed (approvalId "a-1") and decided like this; omitted → nothing proposed. */
    approval?: ApprovalDecision;
    actionFails?: boolean;
    verifyPass?: boolean;
  } = {},
) {
  let t = 1_000_000;
  const steps: string[] = [];
  const requests: LlmRequest[] = [];
  const counters: Record<string, number> = {};
  const recorded: ToolResult[] = [];
  const log: {
    findings?: { f: Findings; mode: string };
    narrated?: boolean;
    finish?: { status: string; stats: unknown };
    ruleArgs?: unknown;
    proposedBy?: string;
    outcome?: { decision: string; detail?: string };
  } = {};
  const replies = [...(o.replies ?? [])];

  const io: RunIO = {
    step: async (name, fn) => {
      steps.push(name);
      return fn();
    },
    now: () => (t += o.tick ?? 100),
    agent: {
      getRunContext: async (runId) => ({
        incidentId: "i",
        runId,
        target: TARGET,
        question: "why is it down?",
        kind: "initial",
        mode: "ai",
        evidence: [],
        scope: { host: HOST, cnameTargets: [] },
        isDemo: false,
        ...o.ctx,
      }),
      addPendingChecks: async (_runId, _turn, calls) =>
        calls.map((c) => {
          const prefix = c.tool === "dns_lookup" ? "dns" : "http";
          counters[prefix] = (counters[prefix] ?? 0) + 1;
          return `${prefix}-${counters[prefix]}`;
        }),
      recordCheck: async (_id, result) => void recorded.push(result),
      ruleFindings: async (_runId, args) => {
        log.ruleArgs = args;
        return salvageFindings(args, { ...RULES, evidence: recorded.slice(-1).map((r) => r.evidenceId) }, recorded.map((r) => r.evidenceId)).findings;
      },
      setFindings: async (_runId, f, mode) => {
        if (o.failSetFindings) throw new Error("D1 down");
        log.findings = { f, mode };
      },
      narrate: async () => void (log.narrated = true),
      finishRun: async (_runId, status, stats) => void (log.finish = { status, stats: { ...stats } }),
      proposeAction: async (_runId, workflowId) => {
        log.proposedBy = workflowId;
        return o.approval ? { approvalId: "a-1", actionId: "restore_demo_service" } : { approvalId: null, actionId: null };
      },
      recordApprovalOutcome: async (_id, decision, detail) => void (log.outcome = { decision, ...(detail ? { detail } : {}) }),
      executeAction: async () => {
        if (o.actionFails) throw new Error("demo unreachable");
        return { ok: true, text: "Restored." };
      },
      afterAction: async () => ({ pass: o.verifyPass ?? true, text: "Verified." }),
    },
    workflowId: "diag-i-1",
    waitForApproval: async () => {
      steps.push("wait-for-approval");
      return o.approval ?? "expired";
    },
    llm: async (req) => {
      requests.push(req);
      const next = replies.shift() ?? reply();
      if (next instanceof Error) throw next;
      return next;
    },
    tool: async (name, _args, evidenceId) => {
      const r = o.tool?.(name, evidenceId) ?? { evidenceId, tool: name, ok: true, status: "fail" as const, summary: `${name} ok`, digest: "{}", raw: name === "dns_lookup" ? { cnameTargets: [] } : {} };
      if (r instanceof Error) throw r;
      return { result: { ...r, evidenceId }, subrequests: name === "dns_lookup" ? 4 : 6 };
    },
  };
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(["http_probe", { url: 42 }]), reply(http()), reply(submit(["http-1"]))] });
    const out = await run(h);
    expect(h.requests[1]!.messages[0]!.content).toContain("Your previous call was invalid: Invalid arguments for http_probe");
    // The invalid call used turn 1, so turn 3 was the forced finish turn.
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ status: "limit_turns", category: "origin_5xx" });
  });

  it("treats a host outside the origin pin as invalid", async () => {
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(["http_probe", { url: "https://evil.example.net/" }]), reply(http()), reply(submit(["http-1"]))] });
    await run(h);
    expect(h.requests[1]!.messages[0]!.content).toMatch(/invalid: .*evil\.example\.net/);
  });

  it("goes to the rules after a second invalid turn, keeping the model's valid fields", async () => {
    const bad = submit(["The site is broken"], { category: "high_latency", cause: "It is slow." });
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(bad), reply(bad)] });
    const out = await run(h);
    expect(h.requests).toHaveLength(2);
    // The rule path fills in the baseline checks, then keeps the model's category and cause.
    expect(h.recorded.map((r) => r.tool)).toEqual(["dns_lookup", "http_probe"]);
    expect(h.log.ruleArgs).toMatchObject({ category: "high_latency" });
    expect(h.log.findings?.f).toMatchObject({ category: "high_latency", cause: "It is slow.", evidence: ["http-1"] });
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ status: "done", mode: "ai" });
    expect(h.log.finish?.stats).toMatchObject({ source: "model+rules" });
  });

  it("rejects findings that cite checks which don't exist", async () => {
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(submit(["http-9"])), reply(http()), reply(submit(["http-1"]))] });
    await run(h);
    expect(h.requests[1]!.messages[0]!.content).toContain("evidence must cite checks from this incident");
  });
});

describe("diagnose: every limit still ends in findings (TDD §8.2)", () => {
  it("LLM unavailable → baseline checks + rules, rule-based mode", async () => {
    const h = harness({ replies: [new LlmUnavailable("failed", "both models down")] });
    const out = await run(h);
    expect(h.recorded.map((r) => r.tool)).toEqual(["dns_lookup", "http_probe"]);
    expect(h.steps).toEqual(["context", "llm-turn-1", "pending-rules", "tool-dns-1", "tool-http-1", "rules", "findings", "propose", "narrate", "finish"]);
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ status: "llm_unavailable", mode: "rule_based", llmTurns: 1 });
    expect(h.log.finish?.status).toBe("llm_unavailable");
    expect(h.log.finish?.stats).toMatchObject({ source: "rules", fallbackTurns: 0 });
  });

  it("only runs the baseline checks this run hasn't done", async () => {
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(http()), new LlmUnavailable("failed", "down")] });
    await run(h);
    expect(h.recorded.map((r) => r.tool)).toEqual(["http_probe", "dns_lookup"]);
  });

  it("tool cap → a finish-only turn, the model's findings, limit_tools", async () => {
    // Cheap A-only lookups (cap 2 each), so the tool cap hits before the subrequest budget.
    const many = reply(...Array.from({ length: 8 }, (): [string, unknown] => ["dns_lookup", { hostname: HOST, types: ["A"] }]));
    const h = harness({ replies: [many, reply(submit(["dns-1", "dns-6"]))] });
    const out = await run(h);
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ status: "limit_tools", toolCalls: 6, category: "origin_5xx" });
    // The model is told its extra calls didn't run, so it doesn't assume their results.
    expect(h.requests[1]!.messages[0]!.content).toContain("Only 6 tool call(s) were left, so 2 call(s) were dropped.");
    expect(offered(h.requests[1]!)).toEqual(["submit_findings"]);
    expect(h.requests[1]!.system).toContain("This is your last turn");
  });

  it("turn cap → the last turn is finish-only, limit_turns", async () => {
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(http("a")), reply(http("b")), reply(submit(["http-2"]))] });
    const out = await run(h);
    expect(offered(h.requests[2]!)).toEqual(["submit_findings"]);
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ status: "limit_turns", llmTurns: 3, category: "origin_5xx" });
  });

  it("a finish-only turn without usable findings still ends in rule findings", async () => {
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(http("a")), reply(http("b")), reply(http("c"))] });
    const out = await run(h);
    expect(h.steps).toContain("rules");
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ status: "limit_turns", category: "inconclusive" });
  });

  it("budget → skips what doesn't fit, finishes with the model's findings, limit_budget; the tail still runs", async () => {
    // 1 context + 8 tail + 3 for turn 1 + 5 for the DNS check = 17 of 22: the http probe (20 cap) can't fit,
    // and 5 left is too little for another tool + turn, so turn 2 is finish-only.
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(dns(), http()), reply(submit(["dns-1"]))] });
    const out = await run(h, { budget: 22 });
    expect(h.recorded.map((r) => r.tool)).toEqual(["dns_lookup"]);
    expect(offered(h.requests[1]!)).toEqual(["submit_findings"]);
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ status: "limit_budget", category: "origin_5xx" });
    expect(h.steps.slice(-4)).toEqual(["findings", "propose", "narrate", "finish"]);
  });

  it("deadline → no more LLM turns; rules keep the evidence so far, limit_deadline", async () => {
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(dns(), http()), reply(submit(["http-1"]))], tick: 25_000 });
    const out = await run(h);
    expect(h.requests).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(h.recorded.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(h.steps).toContain("rules");
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ status: "limit_deadline" });
  });

  it("a check that throws is recorded as an error and the run carries on", async () => {
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(dns(), http()), reply(submit(["dns-1"]))], tool: (name, id) => (name === "http_probe" ? new Error("socket hang up") : { evidenceId: id, tool: name, ok: true, status: "pass", summary: "ok", digest: "{}", raw: {} }) });
    const out = await run(h);
    expect(h.recorded[1]).toMatchObject({ tool: "http_probe", status: "error", summary: "Check failed: socket hang up" });
    expect(out.status).toBe("done");

// Most tests exercise the planning turns themselves, so the baseline checks (WP-13b) are off unless asked for.
const run = (h: ReturnType<typeof harness>, limits: Partial<DiagnoseLimits> = {}) => diagnose(h.io, "run-1", { ...DEFAULT_LIMITS, baseline: false, ...limits });
const offered = (req: LlmRequest) => req.tools!.map((t) => t.function.name);

describe("diagnose: normal runs", () => {
  it("plans, runs checks, takes the model's findings, narrates and finishes", async () => {
    const h = harness({ replies: [reply(dns(), http()), reply(submit(["http-1", "dns-1"]))] });
    const out = await run(h);
    expect(out).toEqual({ status: "done", mode: "ai", toolCalls: 2, llmTurns: 2, category: "origin_5xx" });
    expect(h.steps).toEqual(["context", "llm-turn-1", "pending-1", "tool-dns-1", "tool-http-1", "llm-turn-2", "findings", "propose", "narrate", "finish"]);
    expect(h.log.findings).toEqual({ f: expect.objectContaining({ category: "origin_5xx", evidence: ["http-1", "dns-1"] }), mode: "ai" });
    expect(h.log.finish).toEqual({ status: "done", stats: { toolCalls: 2, llmTurns: 2, fallbackTurns: 0, source: "model" } });
    expect(h.requests[0]).toMatchObject({ toolChoice: "required" });
    expect(offered(h.requests[0]!)).toEqual(["dns_lookup", "http_probe", "cert_info", "provider_status", "submit_findings"]);
    expect(h.requests[1]!.messages[0]!.content).toContain("[http-1] http_probe (fail): http_probe ok");
  });

  it("uses no LLM at all when the run is rule-based", async () => {
    const h = harness({ ctx: { mode: "rule_based" } });
    const out = await run(h);
    expect(h.requests).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(h.recorded.map((r) => r.tool)).toEqual(["dns_lookup", "http_probe"]);
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ status: "done", mode: "rule_based" });
    expect(h.log.findings?.mode).toBe("rule_based");
    expect(h.log.finish?.stats).toMatchObject({ source: "rules", fallbackTurns: 0 });
  });

  it("counts the turns the fallback model answered (WP-20)", async () => {
    const h = harness({ replies: [{ ...reply(http()), fallback: true }, reply(submit(["http-1"]))] });
    await run(h);
    expect(h.log.finish?.stats).toMatchObject({ fallbackTurns: 1, source: "model" });
  });
});

// The integration project's only "internet" (outboundService): DoH answers for *.example.com and the test
// demo host, so Workflow runs are offline and deterministic. Slack and Discord webhooks are recorded: a POST
// is stored and answered by its path (/gone → 404, /flaky → 500 then 204), and GET https://discord.com/__calls
// returns everything recorded so far. nxdomain.example.com doesn't exist,
// broken.example.com returns 500, every other *.example.com host serves a small HTML page, and the test demo
// host is served by the demo stub (through its DEMO binding here).
const DEMO_HOST = "incident-copilot-demo.test.workers.dev";
const fakeInternet = `
const hookCalls = [];
let flaky = 0;
const dns = (body) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { headers: { "content-type": "application/dns-json" } });
export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    if (url.hostname === "cloudflare-dns.com" || url.hostname === "dns.google") {
      const name = (url.searchParams.get("name") || "").replace(/\\.$/, "");
      const known = name.endsWith("example.com") || name === "${DEMO_HOST}";
      if (!known || name === "nxdomain.example.com") return dns({ Status: 3 });
      const a = url.searchParams.get("type") === "A";
      return dns({ Status: 0, Answer: a ? [{ name, type: 1, TTL: 300, data: "93.184.215.14" }] : [] });
    }
    if (url.hostname === "${DEMO_HOST}") return env.DEMO.fetch(request);
    if (url.hostname === "discord.com" || url.hostname === "hooks.slack.com") {
      if (url.pathname === "/__calls") return Response.json(hookCalls);
      hookCalls.push({ host: url.hostname, path: url.pathname, body: await request.json() });
      if (url.pathname.endsWith("/gone")) return new Response("Unknown Webhook", { status: 404 });
      if (url.pathname.endsWith("/flaky") && flaky++ === 0) return new Response("oops", { status: 500 });
      return new Response(null, { status: 204 });
    }
    if (url.hostname === "broken.example.com") return new Response("upstream exploded", { status: 500 });
    if (url.hostname.endsWith("example.com")) return new Response("<html><body>ok</body></html>", { headers: { "content-type": "text/html" } });
    return new Response("fake internet: unknown host " + url.hostname, { status: 502 });
  },
};
`;

export default defineConfig({
  test: {
    projects: [
      {
        test: { name: "unit", include: ["test/unit/**/*.test.ts"], environment: "node" },
      },
      {
        plugins: [
          cloudflareTest(async () => ({
            wrangler: { configPath: "./wrangler.jsonc" },
            // The AI binding is remote by default: tests must not need a login or spend neurons.
            remoteBindings: false,
            miniflare: {
              bindings: {
                TEST_MIGRATIONS: await readD1Migrations("./migrations"),
                LLM_MODE: "mock",
import { introspectWorkflow } from "cloudflare:test";
import { env } from "cloudflare:workers";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { RunMode } from "../../worker/agent/state";
import { incidentAgent } from "../../worker/agent/stub";
import { newId } from "../../worker/lib/ids";

/**
 * Real DiagnosisWorkflow runs: local Workflows, the mock LLM (LLM_MODE=mock) and the fake internet from
 * vitest.config.ts (broken.example.com → 500, ok.example.com → 200, DoH for *.example.com).
 */
async function diagnoseRun(message: string, target: string, mode?: RunMode) {
  const introspector = await introspectWorkflow(env.DIAGNOSIS_WORKFLOW);
  // A broken real site gets an "Add to watchlist" card (WP-18); it expires at once, so the Workflow completes.
  await introspector.modifyAll((m) => m.forceEventTimeout({ name: "wait-for-approval" }));
  try {
    const id = newId();
    const agent = await incidentAgent(env, id);
    const { runId } = await agent.init({ workspaceId: newId(), target, origin: "chat", message, ...(mode ? { mode } : {}) });
    expect(await agent.launchRun(runId!)).toBe(`diag-${id}-1`);
    const [instance] = await introspector.get();
    await instance!.waitForStatus("complete");
    return { id, agent, runId: runId!, output: await instance!.getOutput(), snapshot: await agent.getSnapshot() };
  } finally {
    await introspector.dispose();
  }
}

const finished = (s: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof diagnoseRun>>["snapshot"]) => s.timeline.filter((t) => t.type === "run_finished").map((t) => t.detail.status);

describe("DiagnosisWorkflow (mock LLM, fake internet)", () => {
  it("diagnoses a failing origin end to end", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s, output, runId } = await diagnoseRun("why is broken.example.com down?", "https://broken.example.com/");
    // The baseline DNS + HTTP checks run before the first AI turn (WP-13b), so one turn is enough.
    expect(output).toMatchObject({ status: "done", mode: "ai", toolCalls: 2, llmTurns: 1, category: "origin_5xx" });
    expect(s.checks.map((c) => [c.evidenceId, c.status])).toEqual([
      ["dns-1", "pass"],
      ["http-1", "fail"],
    ]);
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "origin_5xx", status: "investigating", activeRunId: null, mode: "ai" });
    // WP-20: the eval reads who made the findings from the snapshot.
    expect(s.timeline.find((t) => t.type === "run_finished")?.detail).toMatchObject({ status: "done", source: "model", fallbackTurns: 0 });
    expect(s.approvals.map((a) => [a.actionId, a.status])).toEqual([["add_to_watchlist", "expired"]]);
    const narration = s.messages.find((m) => m.id === `narr-${runId}`)!;
    expect(narration.content).toContain("[http-1]");
    expect(finished(s)).toEqual(["done"]);
  });

  it("resolves a healthy site on its first run", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s } = await diagnoseRun("is ok.example.com up?", "https://ok.example.com/");
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "healthy", status: "resolved", activeRunId: null });
  });

  it("a rule_based run (the deploy smoke test) diagnoses the healthy demo without an LLM turn", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s, output, runId } = await diagnoseRun("diagnose demo", `${env.DEMO_BASE_URL}/w/${newId()}/`, "rule_based");
    expect(output).toMatchObject({ status: "done", mode: "rule_based", llmTurns: 0, category: "healthy" });
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "healthy", status: "resolved", mode: "rule_based", activeRunId: null });
    expect(s.checks.map((c) => [c.evidenceId, c.status])).toEqual([
      ["dns-1", "pass"],
      ["http-1", "pass"],
    ]);
    // The narration is the rule template, cited like a model's.
    expect(s.messages.find((m) => m.id === `narr-${runId}`)!.content).toContain("[http-1]");
    expect(finished(s)).toEqual(["done"]);
  });

  it("falls back to the rules when the LLM is down", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s, output } = await diagnoseRun("broken.example.com #llm-down", "https://broken.example.com/");
    expect(output).toMatchObject({ status: "llm_unavailable", mode: "rule_based", llmTurns: 1 });
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "origin_5xx", mode: "rule_based", activeRunId: null });
    expect(s.checks.map((c) => c.tool)).toEqual(["dns_lookup", "http_probe"]);
    expect(s.messages.at(-1)!.content).toContain("- HTTP probe: HTTP 500/500/500");
    expect(finished(s)).toEqual(["llm_unavailable"]);
  });

  it("goes to the rules after two invalid calls", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s, output } = await diagnoseRun("broken.example.com #llm-invalid", "https://broken.example.com/");
    expect(output).toMatchObject({ status: "done", mode: "ai", llmTurns: 2 });
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ category: "origin_5xx", activeRunId: null });
  });

  it("reports a name that doesn't resolve", async () => {
    const { snapshot: s } = await diagnoseRun("nxdomain.example.com is down", "https://nxdomain.example.com/");
    expect(s.category).toBe("dns_failure");
  });

  it("runs a follow-up as a second Workflow that sees the earlier evidence", async () => {
    const first = await diagnoseRun("why is broken.example.com down?", "https://broken.example.com/");
    const introspector = await introspectWorkflow(env.DIAGNOSIS_WORKFLOW);
    await introspector.modifyAll((m) => m.forceEventTimeout({ name: "wait-for-approval" }));
    try {
      const runId = await first.agent.startRun("still broken?", "followup");
      expect(runId).toBe("run-2");
      expect((await first.agent.getRunContext(runId)).evidence.map((e) => e.evidenceId)).toEqual(["dns-1", "http-1"]);
      await first.agent.launchRun(runId);
      const [instance] = await introspector.get();
      await instance!.waitForStatus("complete");
      const s = await first.agent.getSnapshot();
      expect(s.activeRunId).toBeNull();
      expect(s.messages.filter((m) => m.role === "assistant").map((m) => m.id)).toEqual(["narr-run-1", "narr-run-2"]);
    } finally {
      await introspector.dispose();
    }
  });
});

```
import { CATEGORY_INFO, type Category, type Findings } from "../diagnosis/categories";
import { renderTemplate } from "../diagnosis/template";
import type { ToolResult } from "../tools/types";
import { LlmUnavailable, toResult, type ChatCompletion, type LlmRequest, type LlmResult } from "./models";
import type { EvidenceLine } from "./prompts";

/**
 * `LLM_MODE=mock` (TDD §7.4): scripted turns in the recorded chat-completions shape, run through the real
 * mapper, costing 0 neurons. A demo URL doesn't show its mode, so the final turn reads the evidence instead
 * of keying on the target. Test triggers in the question: `#llm-down`, `#llm-invalid`.
 */
export const MOCK_MODEL = "mock";

const LINE = /^\[([a-z]+-\d+)\] (\w+) \((\w+)\): (.*)\n {2}(.*)$/gm;

export function mockCall(req: LlmRequest): LlmResult {
  const user = req.messages.map((m) => m.content).join("\n");
  if (user.includes("#llm-down")) throw new LlmUnavailable("failed", "mock: #llm-down");
  if (req.tools?.length) return toResult(MOCK_MODEL, planningTurn(req.system, user));
  return { text: narration(user), toolCalls: [], usage: { in: 0, out: 0, neurons: 0 }, model: MOCK_MODEL, fallback: false };
}

export function mockStream(req: LlmRequest, onDelta: (text: string) => void): LlmResult {
  const result = mockCall(req);
  const text = result.text ?? "";
  const size = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(text.length / 4));
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += size) onDelta(text.slice(i, i + size));
  return result;
}

function planningTurn(system: string, user: string): ChatCompletion {
  if (user.includes("#llm-invalid")) return completion([{ name: "http_probe", args: "{not json" }]);
  const evidence = parseEvidence(user);
  if (evidence.length === 0) {
    const target = /^Target: (\S+)$/m.exec(system)?.[1] ?? "https://example.com/";
    return completion([
      { name: "dns_lookup", args: JSON.stringify({ hostname: new URL(target).hostname }) },
      { name: "http_probe", args: JSON.stringify({ url: target }) },
    ]);
  }
  const category = pickCategory(evidence);
  const ids = [last(evidence, "http_probe"), last(evidence, "dns_lookup")].flatMap((e) => (e ? [e.evidenceId] : []));
  const findings: Findings = {
    category,
    confidence: category === "inconclusive" ? "low" : "high",
    evidence: ids.length ? ids : [evidence[0]!.evidenceId],
    cause: `Mock diagnosis: ${CATEGORY_INFO[category]}`,
    next_steps: ["Set LLM_MODE=live for a real diagnosis."],
    actions: category === "healthy" ? [] : ["restore_demo_service"],
  };
  return completion([{ name: "submit_findings", args: JSON.stringify(findings) }]);
}

function pickCategory(evidence: EvidenceLine[]): Category {
  const dns = last(evidence, "dns_lookup");
  const http = last(evidence, "http_probe");
  if (dns && /doesn't resolve|No A or AAAA/.test(dns.summary)) return "dns_failure";
  if (http) {
    if (/^(Redirect loop|Too many redirects)/.test(http.summary)) return "redirect_loop";
    if (/^HTTP 5\d\d/.test(http.summary)) return "origin_5xx";
    if (/^Slow:/.test(http.summary)) return "high_latency";
    if (/"asset":\{[^}]*("cacheControl":"[^"]*(no-store|private)|"setCookie":true)/.test(http.digest)) return "cache_misconfiguration";
  }
  if (dns && /^Resolvers disagree/.test(dns.summary)) return "dns_mismatch";
  if (http && /^HTTP [23]\d\d/.test(http.summary)) return "healthy";
  return "inconclusive";
}

/** Four sections from the findings and evidence in the narration prompt, every line cited. */
function narration(user: string): string {
  const json = /^Findings: (.*)$/m.exec(user)?.[1];
  if (!json) return "Mock reply. Set LLM_MODE=live for a real answer.";
  const findings = { actions: [], ...(JSON.parse(json) as Omit<Findings, "actions">) } as Findings;
  const cites = findings.evidence.map((id) => `[${id}]`).join(" ");
  const cause = /\[[a-z]+-\d+\]/.test(findings.cause) ? findings.cause : `${findings.cause.replace(/\.$/, "")} ${cites}.`;
  const results = parseEvidence(user).map((e) => ({ ...e, ok: true, raw: null }) as ToolResult);
  return renderTemplate({ ...findings, cause }, results);
}

export function parseEvidence(text: string): EvidenceLine[] {
  return [...text.matchAll(LINE)].map((m) => ({
    evidenceId: m[1]!,
    tool: m[2] as EvidenceLine["tool"],
    status: m[3] as EvidenceLine["status"],
    summary: m[4]!,
    digest: m[5]!,
  }));
}

const last = (evidence: EvidenceLine[], tool: EvidenceLine["tool"]) => evidence.filter((e) => e.tool === tool).at(-1);

function completion(calls: { name: string; args: string }[]): ChatCompletion {
  return {
    choices: [{ message: { content: null, tool_calls: calls.map((c, i) => ({ id: `mock-call-${i}`, function: { name: c.name, arguments: c.args } })) } }],
    usage: { prompt_tokens: 0, completion_tokens: 0, neurons: 0 },
  };
}
```
